        return float(np.abs(np.sum(pattern1 * np.conj(pattern2))))

    def _create_quantum_chunk(self, data: Any, resonance: float) -> Any:
        """Create data chunk with quantum influence."""
        if isinstance(data, str):
            words = data.split()
            chunk_size = int(len(words) * resonance)
            return " ".join(np.random.choice(words, chunk_size))
        elif isinstance(data, list):
            chunk_size = int(len(data) * resonance)
            return list(np.random.choice(data, chunk_size))
        return data

    def _store_in_field_memory(self, pattern: np.ndarray, results: Dict):
        """Store results in field memory."""
        # Create memory pattern
        memory_pattern = pattern * np.exp(1j * np.angle(self.field_state.phase_field))
        
        # Add to field memory
        self.field_memory = self.field_memory.multiply(0.9) + \
                          csr_matrix(0.1 * memory_pattern)
def recognize_patterns(self, data: Any) -> List[Dict]:
   """Extract patterns using quantum field resonance."""
   # Create quantum representation
   data_pattern = self._create_task_pattern({"data": data})
   
   # Process through quantum field
   field_pattern = self._process_through_field(data_pattern)
   
   # Detect resonances with stored patterns
   patterns = []
   
   # FFT analysis for frequency patterns
   fft_pattern = fft2(field_pattern)
   frequency_components = np.abs(fft_pattern)
   
   # Find peaks in frequency spectrum
   peaks = self._find_spectral_peaks(frequency_components)
   
   # Analyze each peak
   for peak in peaks:
       # Extract pattern properties
       frequency = peak["frequency"]
       amplitude = peak["amplitude"]
       phase = np.angle(fft_pattern[peak["indices"]])
       
       # Calculate pattern significance
       significance = amplitude * self.field_state.coherence
       
       pattern = {
           "type": "quantum_resonance",
           "frequency": frequency,
           "amplitude": amplitude,
           "phase": phase,
           "significance": significance,
           "coherence": self.field_state.coherence
       }
       
       # Add derived properties
       if isinstance(data, str):
           pattern.update(self._analyze_text_pattern(data, frequency))
       elif isinstance(data, (list, np.ndarray)):
           pattern.update(self._analyze_numerical_pattern(data, frequency))
           
       patterns.append(pattern)
       
   # Store in pattern cache with quantum signature
   self.pattern_cache.extend([
       {**p, "quantum_signature": self._generate_quantum_signature(p)}
       for p in patterns
   ])
   
   return patterns

def _find_spectral_peaks(self, spectrum: np.ndarray) -> List[Dict]:
   """Find significant peaks in frequency spectrum."""
   # Calculate threshold based on mean and std
   threshold = np.mean(spectrum) + 2 * np.std(spectrum)
   
   peaks = []
   for i in range(1, spectrum.shape[0]-1):
       for j in range(1, spectrum.shape[1]-1):
           if spectrum[i,j] > threshold:
               if self._is_local_maximum(spectrum, i, j):
                   peaks.append({
                       "indices": (i,j),
                       "frequency": np.sqrt(i*i + j*j),
                       "amplitude": spectrum[i,j]
                   })
   
   return peaks

def _is_local_maximum(self, array: np.ndarray, i: int, j: int) -> bool:
   """Check if point is local maximum in 3x3 neighborhood."""
   neighborhood = array[i-1:i+2, j-1:j+2]
   return array[i,j] == np.max(neighborhood)

def generate_insight(self) -> Dict:
   """Generate unified insight using quantum field coherence."""
   if not self.task_history:
       return {"insight": "Insufficient data for insight generation"}
       
   # Calculate field coherence trend
   coherence_history = [
       task["field_coherence"] for task in self.task_history
   ]
   coherence_trend = np.gradient(coherence_history)
   
   # Analyze pattern evolution
   pattern_evolution = self._analyze_pattern_evolution()
   
   # Generate quantum signature for insight
   quantum_state = {
       "coherence": self.field_state.coherence,
       "energy_density": np.mean(self.field_state.energy_density),
       "phase_alignment": np.mean(np.exp(1j * self.field_state.phase_field))
   }
   
   # Analyze gear performance
   gear_analysis = self._analyze_gear_performance()
   
   # Combine into unified insight
   insight = {
       "tasks_processed": len(self.task_history),
       "unique_patterns": len(self.pattern_cache),
       "field_state": quantum_state,
       "pattern_evolution": pattern_evolution,
       "gear_performance": gear_analysis,
       "coherence_trend": {
           "current": coherence_history[-1],
           "trend": float(coherence_trend[-1]),
           "stability": float(np.std(coherence_history))
       }
   }
   
   # Add quantum verification
   insight["quantum_signature"] = self._generate_quantum_signature(insight)
   
   return insight

def _analyze_pattern_evolution(self) -> Dict:
   """Analyze how patterns evolve over time."""
   if not self.pattern_cache:
       return {}
       
   # Group patterns by type
   pattern_groups = {}
   for pattern in self.pattern_cache:
       p_type = pattern["type"]
       if p_type not in pattern_groups:
           pattern_groups[p_type] = []
       pattern_groups[p_type].append(pattern)
       
   evolution = {}
   for p_type, patterns in pattern_groups.items():
       # Track key metrics over time
       significance_trend = [p["significance"] for p in patterns]
       coherence_trend = [p["coherence"] for p in patterns]
       
       evolution[p_type] = {
           "count": len(patterns),
           "avg_significance": float(np.mean(significance_trend)),
           "significance_trend": float(np.gradient(significance_trend)[-1]),
           "coherence_stability": float(np.std(coherence_trend))
       }
       
   return evolution

def _analyze_gear_performance(self) -> Dict:
   """Analyze performance of processing gears."""
   analysis = {}
   
   for gear_name, state in self.gear_states.items():
       # Calculate key metrics
       efficiency_trend = state.efficiency
       resonance_strength = np.mean(np.abs(state.resonance_pattern))
       entanglement_degree = len(state.entanglement_map)
       
       analysis[gear_name] = {
           "efficiency": float(efficiency_trend),
           "resonance_strength": float(resonance_strength), 
           "entanglement_degree": entanglement_degree,
           "specialization": state.specialization
       }
       
   return analysis

def _generate_quantum_signature(self, data: Dict) -> str:
   """Generate quantum signature for data verification."""
   # Convert data to string representation
   data_str = str(sorted(data.items()))
   
   # Create quantum pattern
   pattern = self._create_task_pattern({"data": data_str})
   
   # Process through current field state
   processed = self._process_through_field(pattern)
   
   # Generate signature from processed pattern
   signature = np.sum(processed * np.exp(1j * self.field_state.phase_field))
   
   return str(signature)

def refine_perspectives(self) -> List[Dict]:
   """Refine perspectives using quantum resonance matching."""
   refined_perspectives = []
   
   # Get current field state signature
   field_signature = self._generate_quantum_signature({
       "field_state": self.field_state.energy_density
   })
   
   for perspective in self.perspective_repository:
       # Generate perspective signature
       perspective_signature = self._generate_quantum_signature(perspective)
       
       # Calculate resonance between signatures
       resonance = self._calculate_signature_resonance(
           field_signature, 
           perspective_signature
       )
       
       if resonance > self.coherence_threshold:
           # Refine perspective through quantum field
           refined = {
               "source": perspective,
               "resonance": float(resonance),
               "refinement": self._apply_quantum_refinement(perspective),
               "quantum_signature": perspective_signature
           }
           refined_perspectives.append(refined)
           
   return refined_perspectives

def _calculate_signature_resonance(self, sig1: str, sig2: str) -> float:
   """Calculate resonance between quantum signatures."""
   # Convert signatures to complex numbers
   c1 = complex(sig1)
   c2 = complex(sig2)
   
   # Calculate phase alignment
   phase_diff = np.angle(c1) - np.angle(c2)
   
   # Combine amplitude and phase alignment
   resonance = np.abs(c1) * np.abs(c2) * np.cos(phase_diff)
   
   return float(resonance)

def _apply_quantum_refinement(self, perspective: Dict) -> Dict:
   """Apply quantum field refinement to perspective."""
   # Create quantum pattern from perspective
   pattern = self._create_task_pattern({"data": perspective})
   
   # Process through quantum field
   refined_pattern = self._process_through_field(pattern)
   
   # Convert back to insight space
   refined_data = ifft2(refined_pattern).real
   
   # Extract refined perspective
   return {
       "field_coherence": float(self.field_state.coherence),
       "refinement_strength": float(np.mean(np.abs(refined_pattern))),
       "refined_data": refined_data.tolist()
   }

# KaleidoscopeLogic.py

import numpy as np

class KaleidoscopeLogic:
    """
    Implements dynamic logic shifting for nodes in the Kaleidoscope AI system.
    Mimics the shifting perspectives of a kaleidoscope to adapt logic paths dynamically.
    """

    def __init__(self, dimensions=5, memory_capacity=100):
        self.dimensions = dimensions  # Number of interconnected logic "gears"
        self.memory = [np.zeros(memory_capacity) for _ in range(dimensions)]  # Memory banks
        self.weights = np.random.rand(dimensions, dimensions)  # Gear interconnection weights
        self.current_path = []  # Tracks active logic paths

    def process_memory(self, input_data):
        """
        Fills memory banks with input data and calculates new logic paths.
        :param input_data: List of input values corresponding to memory dimensions.
        """
        for i, value in enumerate(input_data):
            if i < self.dimensions:
                self.memory[i] += value * 0.01
        self.current_path = self._calculate_logic_path()

    def _calculate_logic_path(self):
        """
        Determines the active logic path based on memory fills and interconnection weights.
        :return: List of indices representing the active logic path.
        """
        active_gears = []
        for i, memory_bank in enumerate(self.memory):
            if np.sum(memory_bank) > np.mean(self.weights[i]):
                active_gears.append(i)
        return active_gears

    def shift_logic(self):
        """
        Shifts logic weights dynamically based on the current path.
        """
        for i in self.current_path:
            self.weights[i] += np.random.rand(self.dimensions) * 0.01
        self.weights = np.clip(self.weights, 0, 1)  # Ensure weights stay within bounds

    def visualize_state(self):
        """
        Prints the current state of the Kaleidoscope Logic Engine.
        """
        print("Current Weights:", self.weights)
        print("Memory Sums:", [np.sum(mem) for mem in self.memory])
        print("Active Logic Path:", self.current_path)

# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    logic_engine = KaleidoscopeLogic(dimensions=5, memory_capacity=100)

    # Input data simulation
    input_data = [10, 20, 5, 15, 25]
    logic_engine.process_memory(input_data)

    # Shift logic based on input
    logic_engine.shift_logic()

    # Visualize current state
    logic_engine.visualize_state()

    logic_engine.visualize_state()

# logical_reasoning.py

# File: logical_reasoning.py
# Description: Adds logical reasoning capabilities to nodes.

class LogicalReasoningNode(MemoryEnhancedNode):
    """Extends MemoryEnhancedNode with logical reasoning capabilities."""
    
    def analyze_patterns(self):
        """Analyzes knowledge for logical patterns."""
        if len(self.dna["knowledge"]) < 2:
            self.logs.append("Insufficient knowledge for logical analysis.")
            return "No patterns identified."

        # Simplified pattern recognition
        patterns = [f"Pattern_{i}: {item}" for i, item in enumerate(self.dna["knowledge"])]
        self.logs.append(f"Identified patterns: {patterns}")
        return patterns

    def deduce(self):
        """Makes a logical deduction based on patterns."""
        patterns = self.analyze_patterns()
        if patterns == "No patterns identified.":
            deduction = "No deduction possible."
        else:
            deduction = f"Deduction: {patterns[0]} leads to deeper insight."
        self.logs.append(f"Deduction made: {deduction}")
        return deduction


# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Create a logical reasoning node
    logic_node = LogicalReasoningNode()

    # Simulate learning and reasoning
    inputs = ["Data_1", "Data_2", "Data_3"]
    for input_data in inputs:
        logic_node.perceive(input_data)

    # Perform logical reasoning
    deduction = logic_node.deduce()

    # Display node status and deduction
    print("\n--- Logical Reasoning Node Status ---")
    print(logic_node.status())
    print("\n--- Deduction ---")
    print(deduction)


# logic-gears.py

from __future__ import annotations
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Set
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import asyncio
import logging
from datetime import datetime

from ..nodes.base_node import BaseNode
from ..memory.pathways import PathwayManager
from ...utils.validation import validate_gear_operation

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

@dataclass
class LogicGear:
    gear_id: str
    specialization: str
    weight: float = 1.0
    active: bool = True
    connected_gears: Set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    processing_queue: asyncio.Queue = field(default_factory=asyncio.Queue)
    operations_count: int = 0
    last_active: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)

class LogicGearSystem:
    def __init__(
        self,
        pathway_manager: PathwayManager,
        num_gears: int = 40
    ):
        self.pathway_manager = pathway_manager
        self.gears: Dict[str, LogicGear] = {}
        self.gear_connections: Dict[str, Set[str]] = {}
        self._initialize_gears(num_gears)

    def _initialize_gears(self, num_gears: int):
        specializations = [
            "pattern_recognition",
            "data_transformation",
            "memory_integration",
            "decision_making",
            "learning_optimization"
        ]
        
        for i in range(num_gears):
            gear_id = f"gear_{i}"
            spec = specializations[i % len(specializations)]
            
            self.gears[gear_id] = LogicGear(
                gear_id=gear_id,
                specialization=spec,
                weight=np.random.uniform(0.5, 1.5)
            )
        
        self._establish_gear_connections()

    def _establish_gear_connections(self):
        """Creates initial connections between gears."""
        gear_ids = list(self.gears.keys())
        
        for gear_id in gear_ids:
            # Connect each gear to 2-4 others
            num_connections = np.random.randint(2, 5)
            potential_connections = [id for id in gear_ids if id != gear_id]
            
            if potential_connections:
                connections = set(np.random.choice(
                    potential_connections,
                    size=min(num_connections, len(potential_connections)),
                    replace=False
                ))
                
                self.gears[gear_id].connected_gears = connections
                
                # Create reciprocal connections
                for connected_id in connections:
                    self.gears[connected_id].connected_gears.add(gear_id)

    async def process_data(
        self,
        data: Dict[str, Any],
        source_gear: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Processes data through the gear system."""
        if not source_gear:
            source_gear = self._select_entry_gear(data)

        processed_results = []
        visited_gears = set()
        
        async def process_through_gear(gear_id: str, current_data: Dict):
            if gear_id in visited_gears:
                return
            
            visited_gears.add(gear_id)
            gear = self.gears[gear_id]
            
            # Process data through current gear
            result = await self._process_in_gear(gear, current_data)
            if result:
                processed_results.append(result)
                
                # Propagate to connected gears
                for connected_id in gear.connected_gears:
                    if connected_id not in visited_gears:
                        await process_through_gear(connected_id, result)

        # Start processing from source gear
        await process_through_gear(source_gear, data)
        return processed_results

    async def _process_in_gear(
        self,
        gear: LogicGear,
        data: Dict[str, Any]
    ) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Processes data within a specific gear."""
        try:
            # Apply gear's specialization
            if gear.specialization == "pattern_recognition":
                result = await self._recognize_patterns(gear, data)
            elif gear.specialization == "data_transformation":
                result = await self._transform_data(gear, data)
            elif gear.specialization == "memory_integration":
                result = await self._integrate_memory(gear, data)
            elif gear.specialization == "decision_making":
                result = await self._make_decision(gear, data)
            elif gear.specialization == "learning_optimization":
                result = await self._optimize_learning(gear, data)
            else:
                result = await self._default_processing(gear, data)

            if result:
                # Update gear metrics
                gear.operations_count += 1
                gear.last_active = datetime.now()
                
                # Validate result
                if validate_gear_operation(result):
                    return result
                    
            return None

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Gear processing error in {gear.gear_id}: {str(e)}")
            return None

    async def _recognize_patterns(
        self,
        gear: LogicGear,
        data: Dict[str, Any]
    ) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Recognizes patterns in data."""
        patterns = []
        confidence_scores = []
        
        try:
            # Extract numerical patterns
            if isinstance(data.get("values"), (list, np.ndarray)):
                num_pattern = self._find_numerical_patterns(data["values"])
                if num_pattern:
                    patterns.append(num_pattern)
                    confidence_scores.append(num_pattern["confidence"])

            # Extract structural patterns
            if isinstance(data.get("structure"), dict):
                struct_pattern = self._find_structural_patterns(data["structure"])
                if struct_pattern:
                    patterns.append(struct_pattern)
                    confidence_scores.append(struct_pattern["confidence"])

            # Extract temporal patterns
            if "timestamp" in data:
                temp_pattern = self._find_temporal_patterns(data)
                if temp_pattern:
                    patterns.append(temp_pattern)
                    confidence_scores.append(temp_pattern["confidence"])

            if patterns:
                return {
                    "type": "pattern_recognition",
                    "gear_id": gear.gear_id,
                    "patterns": patterns,
                    "confidence": np.mean(confidence_scores),
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                }

            return None

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Pattern recognition error: {str(e)}")
            return None

    async def _transform_data(
        self,
        gear: LogicGear,
        data: Dict[str, Any]
    ) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Transforms data using the gear's specialization."""
        try:
            transformed_data = {}
            
            # Transform numerical values
            if isinstance(data.get("values"), (list, np.ndarray)):
                values = np.array(data["values"])
                transformed_data["normalized"] = self._normalize_values(values)
                transformed_data["encoded"] = self._encode_values(values)

            # Transform structures
            if isinstance(data.get("structure"), dict):
                transformed_data["structure"] = self._transform_structure(
                    data["structure"]
                )

            # Transform sequences
            if isinstance(data.get("sequence"), list):
                transformed_data["sequence"] = self._transform_sequence(
                    data["sequence"]
                )

            return {
                "type": "data_transformation",
                "gear_id": gear.gear_id,
                "transformed_data": transformed_data,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            }

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Data transformation error: {str(e)}")
            return None

    async def _integrate_memory(
        self,
        gear: LogicGear,
        data: Dict[str, Any]
    ) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Integrates data with memory system."""
        try:
            # Create memory pathways
            pathway_ids = []
            for key, value in data.items():
                if isinstance(value, dict):
                    pathway_id = await self.pathway_manager.create_pathway(
                        source_id=gear.gear_id,
                        target_id="memory_bank",
                        pathway_type="integration",
                        weight=gear.weight
                    )
                    if pathway_id:
                        pathway_ids.append(pathway_id)

            # Send data through pathways
            integration_results = []
            for pathway_id in pathway_ids:
                result = await self.pathway_manager.send_data(
                    data=data,
                    source_id=gear.gear_id,
                    target_id="memory_bank",
                    priority=gear.weight
                )
                if result:
                    integration_results.append(result)

            return {
                "type": "memory_integration",
                "gear_id": gear.gear_id,
                "pathways": pathway_ids,
                "integration_results": integration_results,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            }

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Memory integration error: {str(e)}")
            return None

    async def _make_decision(
        self,
        gear: LogicGear,
        data: Dict[str, Any]
    ) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Makes decisions based on processed data."""
        try:
            decisions = []
            confidences = []

            # Analyze available evidence
            evidence = self._gather_evidence(data)
            if evidence:
                # Generate decision candidates
                candidates = self._generate_decision_candidates(evidence)
                
                # Evaluate each candidate
                for candidate in candidates:
                    evaluation = self._evaluate_decision(candidate, evidence)
                    if evaluation["confidence"] > 0.5:
                        decisions.append(evaluation["decision"])
                        confidences.append(evaluation["confidence"])

            if decisions:
                return {
                    "type": "decision_making",
                    "gear_id": gear.gear_id,
                    "decisions": decisions,
                    "confidence": np.mean(confidences),
                    "evidence": evidence,
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                }

            return None

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Decision making error: {str(e)}")
            return None

    async def _optimize_learning(
        self,
        gear: LogicGear,
        data: Dict[str, Any]
    ) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Optimizes learning parameters based on processing results."""
        try:
            # Extract performance metrics
            metrics = self._extract_performance_metrics(data)
            
            # Calculate optimal parameters
            optimized_params = self._calculate_optimal_parameters(metrics)
            
            # Generate optimization directives
            directives = []
            for param, value in optimized_params.items():
                if self._should_apply_optimization(param, value, metrics):
                    directives.append({
                        "parameter": param,
                        "value": value,
                        "confidence": metrics.get(f"{param}_confidence", 0.5)
                    })

            if directives:
                return {
                    "type": "learning_optimization",
                    "gear_id": gear.gear_id,
                    "directives": directives,
                    "metrics": metrics,
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                }

            return None

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Learning optimization error: {str(e)}")
            return None

    def _select_entry_gear(self, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        """Selects the most appropriate gear for initial processing."""
        try:
            # Calculate suitability scores
            scores = {}
            for gear_id, gear in self.gears.items():
                score = self._calculate_gear_suitability(gear, data)
                scores[gear_id] = score

            # Select gear with highest score
            return max(scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1])[0]

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Entry gear selection error: {str(e)}")
            return list(self.gears.keys())[0]

    def _calculate_gear_suitability(
        self,
        gear: LogicGear,
        data: Dict[str, Any]
    ) -> float:
        """Calculates how suitable a gear is for processing specific data."""
        try:
            score = gear.weight
            
            # Check specialization match
            if gear.specialization == "pattern_recognition":
                if "values" in data or "sequence" in data:
                    score *= 1.5
            elif gear.specialization == "data_transformation":
                if "values" in data or "structure" in data:
                    score *= 1.5
            elif gear.specialization == "memory_integration":
                if "structure" in data or "references" in data:
                    score *= 1.5
            elif gear.specialization == "decision_making":
                if "evidence" in data or "options" in data:
                    score *= 1.5
            elif gear.specialization == "learning_optimization":
                if "metrics" in data or "performance" in data:
                    score *= 1.5

            # Consider gear activity
            time_since_active = (datetime.now() - gear.last_active).total_seconds()
            activity_factor = 1.0 / (1.0 + time_since_active / 3600)  # 1-hour scale
            score *= activity_factor

            return score

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Gear suitability calculation error: {str(e)}")
            return 0.0
# matrixutils.py

"""Utilities to deal with sympy.Matrix, numpy and scipy.sparse."""

from sympy.core.expr import Expr
from sympy.core.numbers import I
from sympy.core.singleton import S
from sympy.matrices.matrixbase import MatrixBase
from sympy.matrices import eye, zeros
from sympy.external import import_module

__all__ = [
    'numpy_ndarray',
    'scipy_sparse_matrix',
    'sympy_to_numpy',
    'sympy_to_scipy_sparse',
    'numpy_to_sympy',
    'scipy_sparse_to_sympy',
    'flatten_scalar',
    'matrix_dagger',
    'to_sympy',
    'to_numpy',
    'to_scipy_sparse',
    'matrix_tensor_product',
    'matrix_zeros'
]

# Conditionally define the base classes for numpy and scipy.sparse arrays
# for use in isinstance tests.

np = import_module('numpy')
if not np:
    class numpy_ndarray:
        pass
else:
    numpy_ndarray = np.ndarray  # type: ignore

scipy = import_module('scipy', import_kwargs={'fromlist': ['sparse']})
if not scipy:
    class scipy_sparse_matrix:
        pass
    sparse = None
else:
    sparse = scipy.sparse
    scipy_sparse_matrix = sparse.spmatrix # type: ignore


def sympy_to_numpy(m, **options):
    """Convert a SymPy Matrix/complex number to a numpy matrix or scalar."""
    if not np:
        raise ImportError
    dtype = options.get('dtype', 'complex')
    if isinstance(m, MatrixBase):
        return np.array(m.tolist(), dtype=dtype)
    elif isinstance(m, Expr):
        if m.is_Number or m.is_NumberSymbol or m == I:
            return complex(m)
    raise TypeError('Expected MatrixBase or complex scalar, got: %r' % m)


def sympy_to_scipy_sparse(m, **options):
    """Convert a SymPy Matrix/complex number to a numpy matrix or scalar."""
    if not np or not sparse:
        raise ImportError
    dtype = options.get('dtype', 'complex')
    if isinstance(m, MatrixBase):
        return sparse.csr_matrix(np.array(m.tolist(), dtype=dtype))
    elif isinstance(m, Expr):
        if m.is_Number or m.is_NumberSymbol or m == I:
            return complex(m)
    raise TypeError('Expected MatrixBase or complex scalar, got: %r' % m)


def scipy_sparse_to_sympy(m, **options):
    """Convert a scipy.sparse matrix to a SymPy matrix."""
    return MatrixBase(m.todense())


def numpy_to_sympy(m, **options):
    """Convert a numpy matrix to a SymPy matrix."""
    return MatrixBase(m)


def to_sympy(m, **options):
    """Convert a numpy/scipy.sparse matrix to a SymPy matrix."""
    if isinstance(m, MatrixBase):
        return m
    elif isinstance(m, numpy_ndarray):
        return numpy_to_sympy(m)
    elif isinstance(m, scipy_sparse_matrix):
        return scipy_sparse_to_sympy(m)
    elif isinstance(m, Expr):
        return m
    raise TypeError('Expected sympy/numpy/scipy.sparse matrix, got: %r' % m)


def to_numpy(m, **options):
    """Convert a sympy/scipy.sparse matrix to a numpy matrix."""
    dtype = options.get('dtype', 'complex')
    if isinstance(m, (MatrixBase, Expr)):
        return sympy_to_numpy(m, dtype=dtype)
    elif isinstance(m, numpy_ndarray):
        return m
    elif isinstance(m, scipy_sparse_matrix):
        return m.todense()
    raise TypeError('Expected sympy/numpy/scipy.sparse matrix, got: %r' % m)


def to_scipy_sparse(m, **options):
    """Convert a sympy/numpy matrix to a scipy.sparse matrix."""
    dtype = options.get('dtype', 'complex')
    if isinstance(m, (MatrixBase, Expr)):
        return sympy_to_scipy_sparse(m, dtype=dtype)
    elif isinstance(m, numpy_ndarray):
        if not sparse:
            raise ImportError
        return sparse.csr_matrix(m)
    elif isinstance(m, scipy_sparse_matrix):
        return m
    raise TypeError('Expected sympy/numpy/scipy.sparse matrix, got: %r' % m)


def flatten_scalar(e):
    """Flatten a 1x1 matrix to a scalar, return larger matrices unchanged."""
    if isinstance(e, MatrixBase):
        if e.shape == (1, 1):
            e = e[0]
    if isinstance(e, (numpy_ndarray, scipy_sparse_matrix)):
        if e.shape == (1, 1):
            e = complex(e[0, 0])
    return e


def matrix_dagger(e):
    """Return the dagger of a sympy/numpy/scipy.sparse matrix."""
    if isinstance(e, MatrixBase):
        return e.H
    elif isinstance(e, (numpy_ndarray, scipy_sparse_matrix)):
        return e.conjugate().transpose()
    raise TypeError('Expected sympy/numpy/scipy.sparse matrix, got: %r' % e)


# TODO: Move this into sympy.matricies.
def _sympy_tensor_product(*matrices):
    """Compute the kronecker product of a sequence of SymPy Matrices.
    """
    from sympy.matrices.expressions.kronecker import matrix_kronecker_product

    return matrix_kronecker_product(*matrices)


def _numpy_tensor_product(*product):
    """numpy version of tensor product of multiple arguments."""
    if not np:
        raise ImportError
    answer = product[0]
    for item in product[1:]:
        answer = np.kron(answer, item)
    return answer


def _scipy_sparse_tensor_product(*product):
    """scipy.sparse version of tensor product of multiple arguments."""
    if not sparse:
        raise ImportError
    answer = product[0]
    for item in product[1:]:
        answer = sparse.kron(answer, item)
    # The final matrices will just be multiplied, so csr is a good final
    # sparse format.
    return sparse.csr_matrix(answer)


def matrix_tensor_product(*product):
    """Compute the matrix tensor product of sympy/numpy/scipy.sparse matrices."""
    if isinstance(product[0], MatrixBase):
        return _sympy_tensor_product(*product)
    elif isinstance(product[0], numpy_ndarray):
        return _numpy_tensor_product(*product)
    elif isinstance(product[0], scipy_sparse_matrix):
        return _scipy_sparse_tensor_product(*product)


def _numpy_eye(n):
    """numpy version of complex eye."""
    if not np:
        raise ImportError
    return np.array(np.eye(n, dtype='complex'))


def _scipy_sparse_eye(n):
    """scipy.sparse version of complex eye."""
    if not sparse:
        raise ImportError
    return sparse.eye(n, n, dtype='complex')


def matrix_eye(n, **options):
    """Get the version of eye and tensor_product for a given format."""
    format = options.get('format', 'sympy')
    if format == 'sympy':
        return eye(n)
    elif format == 'numpy':
        return _numpy_eye(n)
    elif format == 'scipy.sparse':
        return _scipy_sparse_eye(n)
    raise NotImplementedError('Invalid format: %r' % format)


def _numpy_zeros(m, n, **options):
    """numpy version of zeros."""
    dtype = options.get('dtype', 'float64')
    if not np:
        raise ImportError
    return np.zeros((m, n), dtype=dtype)


def _scipy_sparse_zeros(m, n, **options):
    """scipy.sparse version of zeros."""
    spmatrix = options.get('spmatrix', 'csr')
    dtype = options.get('dtype', 'float64')
    if not sparse:
        raise ImportError
    if spmatrix == 'lil':
        return sparse.lil_matrix((m, n), dtype=dtype)
    elif spmatrix == 'csr':
        return sparse.csr_matrix((m, n), dtype=dtype)


def matrix_zeros(m, n, **options):
    """"Get a zeros matrix for a given format."""
    format = options.get('format', 'sympy')
    if format == 'sympy':
        return zeros(m, n)
    elif format == 'numpy':
        return _numpy_zeros(m, n, **options)
    elif format == 'scipy.sparse':
        return _scipy_sparse_zeros(m, n, **options)
    raise NotImplementedError('Invaild format: %r' % format)


def _numpy_matrix_to_zero(e):
    """Convert a numpy zero matrix to the zero scalar."""
    if not np:
        raise ImportError
    test = np.zeros_like(e)
    if np.allclose(e, test):
        return 0.0
    else:
        return e


def _scipy_sparse_matrix_to_zero(e):
    """Convert a scipy.sparse zero matrix to the zero scalar."""
    if not np:
        raise ImportError
    edense = e.todense()
    test = np.zeros_like(edense)
    if np.allclose(edense, test):
        return 0.0
    else:
        return e


def matrix_to_zero(e):
    """Convert a zero matrix to the scalar zero."""
    if isinstance(e, MatrixBase):
        if zeros(*e.shape) == e:
            e = S.Zero
    elif isinstance(e, numpy_ndarray):
        e = _numpy_matrix_to_zero(e)
    elif isinstance(e, scipy_sparse_matrix):
        e = _scipy_sparse_matrix_to_zero(e)
    return e

# metrics_tracker.py

# File: system_monitoring/metrics_tracker.py

import logging
from typing import Dict, Any
import psutil  # For system-level metrics

# Configure logging for Metrics Tracker
logging.basicConfig(
    filename="kernal_metrics_tracker.log",
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s"
)

class MetricsTracker:
    """
    Tracks and reports system performance and node metrics.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        """
        Initialize the Metrics Tracker.
        """
        self.node_metrics: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
        logging.info("Kernal initialized the Metrics Tracker.")

    def update_node_metrics(self, node_id: str, energy: float, status: str):
        """
        Updates metrics for a specific node.

        Args:
            node_id (str): Unique identifier for the node.
            energy (float): Current energy level of the node.
            status (str): Status of the node (e.g., active, inactive).
        """
        self.node_metrics[node_id] = {
            "energy": energy,
            "status": status
        }
        logging.info(f"Kernal updated metrics for Node {node_id}: Energy={energy}, Status={status}")

    def get_node_metrics(self) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Retrieves all node metrics.

        Returns:
            Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]: Metrics for all nodes.
        """
        logging.info("Kernal retrieved all node metrics.")
        return self.node_metrics

    def get_system_metrics(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Retrieves system-level performance metrics.

        Returns:
            Dict[str, Any]: Metrics including CPU usage, memory usage, and available disk space.
        """
        system_metrics = {
            "cpu_usage": psutil.cpu_percent(interval=1),
            "memory_usage": psutil.virtual_memory().percent,
            "disk_space": psutil.disk_usage('/').percent,
            "active_nodes": len([n for n in self.node_metrics.values() if n["status"] == "active"]),
            "inactive_nodes": len([n for n in self.node_metrics.values() if n["status"] == "inactive"]),
        }
        logging.info(f"Kernal retrieved system metrics: {system_metrics}")
        return system_metrics


# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    tracker = MetricsTracker()
    tracker.update_node_metrics("Node_1", 75.0, "active")
    tracker.update_node_metrics("Node_2", 10.0, "inactive")
    print("Node Metrics:", tracker.get_node_metrics())
    print("System Metrics:", tracker.get_system_metrics())


# mirrored-engine.py

from __future__ import annotations
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Set, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import asyncio
import logging
from datetime import datetime
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor

from .kaleidoscope_engine import KaleidoscopeEngine
from ..nodes.pure_node import PureNode
from ...memory.graph import MemoryGraph

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

@dataclass
class ProcessingMirror:
    mirror_id: str
    engine: KaleidoscopeEngine
    active_tasks: Set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    results_cache: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    last_sync: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    sync_status: float = 1.0

class MirroredEngine:
    def __init__(
        self,
        num_mirrors: int = 3,
        memory_graph: Optional[MemoryGraph] = None
    ):
        self.mirrors: Dict[str, ProcessingMirror] = {}
        self.memory_graph = memory_graph or MemoryGraph()
        self.thread_pool = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_mirrors * 2)
        self.validation_threshold = 0.8
        
        # Initialize processing mirrors
        for i in range(num_mirrors):
            self._create_mirror(f"mirror_{i}")

    def _create_mirror(self, mirror_id: str):
        engine = KaleidoscopeEngine(
            num_logic_gears=40,
            memory_graph=self.memory_graph
        )
        self.mirrors[mirror_id] = ProcessingMirror(
            mirror_id=mirror_id,
            engine=engine
        )

    async def process_data(
        self,
        data: Dict[str, Any],
        validation_required: bool = True
    ) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        task_id = f"task_{datetime.now().timestamp()}"
        mirror_results = []

        # Process in all mirrors
        for mirror in self.mirrors.values():
            mirror.active_tasks.add(task_id)
            try:
                result = await self._process_in_mirror(mirror, data)
                if result:
                    mirror_results.append((mirror.mirror_id, result))
            finally:
                mirror.active_tasks.remove(task_id)

        if not mirror_results:
            return None

        # Validate and combine results
        if validation_required and len(mirror_results) > 1:
            return await self._validate_results(mirror_results)
        else:
            return mirror_results[0][1]

    async def _process_in_mirror(
        self,
        mirror: ProcessingMirror,
        data: Dict[str, Any]
    ) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        try:
            result = await mirror.engine.process_data(data)
            if result:
                mirror.results_cache[str(hash(str(data)))] = result
                mirror.last_sync = datetime.now()
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Mirror processing error in {mirror.mirror_id}: {str(e)}")
            mirror.sync_status *= 0.9
            return None

    async def _validate_results(
        self,
        results: List[Tuple[str, Dict[str, Any]]]
    ) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        try:
            # Calculate similarity scores
            similarity_matrix = np.zeros((len(results), len(results)))
            for i, (_, result1) in enumerate(results):
                for j, (_, result2) in enumerate(results):
                    if i != j:
                        similarity_matrix[i][j] = self._calculate_similarity(
                            result1,
                            result2
                        )

            # Find most consistent result
            avg_similarities = np.mean(similarity_matrix, axis=1)
            most_consistent_idx = np.argmax(avg_similarities)
            
            if avg_similarities[most_consistent_idx] >= self.validation_threshold:
                validated_result = results[most_consistent_idx][1]
                await self._update_mirror_states(
                    results,
                    most_consistent_idx,
                    avg_similarities
                )
                return validated_result
            else:
                logger.warning("Validation failed: insufficient result consistency")
                return await self._handle_validation_failure(results)

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Result validation error: {str(e)}")
            return results[0][1]  # Return first result as fallback

    def _calculate_similarity(
        self,
        result1: Dict[str, Any],
        result2: Dict[str, Any]
    ) -> float:
        try:
            if not result1 or not result2:
                return 0.0

            similarities = []
            
            # Compare processed data
            if "processed_data" in result1 and "processed_data" in result2:
                data_similarity = self._compare_data_structures(
                    result1["processed_data"],
                    result2["processed_data"]
                )
                similarities.append(data_similarity)

            # Compare insights
            if "insights" in result1 and "insights" in result2:
                insight_similarity = self._compare_insights(
                    result1["insights"],
                    result2["insights"]
                )
                similarities.append(insight_similarity)

            # Compare metrics
            if "metrics" in result1 and "metrics" in result2:
                metric_similarity = self._compare_metrics(
                    result1["metrics"],
                    result2["metrics"]
                )
                similarities.append(metric_similarity)

            return np.mean(similarities) if similarities else 0.0

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Similarity calculation error: {str(e)}")
            return 0.0

    def _compare_data_structures(
        self,
        data1: Any,
        data2: Any,
        depth: int = 0,
        max_depth: int = 5
    ) -> float:
        if depth >= max_depth:
            return 1.0 if data1 == data2 else 0.0

        if type(data1) != type(data2):
            return 0.0

        if isinstance(data1, dict):
            if not data1 or not data2:
                return 1.0 if data1 == data2 else 0.0
                
            similarities = []
            for key in set(data1.keys()) & set(data2.keys()):
                similarity = self._compare_data_structures(
                    data1[key],
                    data2[key],
                    depth + 1
                )
                similarities.append(similarity)
            return np.mean(similarities) if similarities else 0.0

        elif isinstance(data1, (list, tuple)):
            if not data1 or not data2:
                return 1.0 if data1 == data2 else 0.0
                
            similarities = []
            for item1, item2 in zip(data1, data2):
                similarity = self._compare_data_structures(
                    item1,
                    item2,
                    depth + 1
                )
                similarities.append(similarity)
            return np.mean(similarities) if similarities else 0.0

        elif isinstance(data1, (int, float)):
            try:
                diff = abs(data1 - data2)
                max_val = max(abs(data1), abs(data2))
                return 1.0 - (diff / max_val) if max_val > 0 else 1.0
            except:
                return 0.0

        else:
            return 1.0 if data1 == data2 else 0.0

    def _compare_insights(
        self,
        insights1: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        insights2: List[Dict[str, Any]]
    ) -> float:
        if not insights1 or not insights2:
            return 1.0 if insights1 == insights2 else 0.0

        # Match insights by ID or content similarity
        matched_pairs = []
        unmatched1 = set(range(len(insights1)))
        unmatched2 = set(range(len(insights2)))

        # First pass: match by ID
        for i in unmatched1.copy():
            for j in unmatched2.copy():
                if insights1[i].get("id") == insights2[j].get("id"):
                    matched_pairs.append((i, j))
                    unmatched1.remove(i)
                    unmatched2.remove(j)
                    break

        # Second pass: match by content similarity
        for i in unmatched1.copy():
            best_match = None
            best_similarity = 0.0
            for j in unmatched2:
                similarity = self._compare_data_structures(
                    insights1[i],
                    insights2[j]
                )
                if similarity > best_similarity and similarity > 0.8:
                    best_similarity = similarity
                    best_match = j
            
            if best_match is not None:
                matched_pairs.append((i, best_match))
                unmatched1.remove(i)
                unmatched2.remove(best_match)

        # Calculate overall similarity
        if not matched_pairs:
            return 0.0

        similarities = []
        for i, j in matched_pairs:
            similarity = self._compare_data_structures(
                insights1[i],
                insights2[j]
            )
            similarities.append(similarity)

        return np.mean(similarities)

    def _compare_metrics(
        self,
        metrics1: Dict[str, Any],
        metrics2: Dict[str, Any]
    ) -> float:
        if not metrics1 or not metrics2:
            return 1.0 if metrics1 == metrics2 else 0.0

        similarities = []
        common_keys = set(metrics1.keys()) & set(metrics2.keys())
        
        for key in common_keys:
            value1 = metrics1[key]
            value2 = metrics2[key]
            
            if isinstance(value1, (int, float)) and isinstance(value2, (int, float)):
                try:
                    max_val = max(abs(value1), abs(value2))
                    if max_val == 0:
                        similarities.append(1.0 if value1 == value2 else 0.0)
                    else:
                        diff = abs(value1 - value2)
                        similarities.append(1.0 - (diff / max_val))
                except:
                    similarities.append(0.0)
            else:
                similarities.append(
                    1.0 if value1 == value2 else 0.0
                )

        return np.mean(similarities) if similarities else 0.0

    async def _update_mirror_states(
        self,
        results: List[Tuple[str, Dict[str, Any]]],
        validated_idx: int,
        similarities: np.ndarray
    ):
        validated_mirror_id = results[validated_idx][0]
        
        for i, (mirror_id, _) in enumerate(results):
            mirror = self.mirrors[mirror_id]
            if mirror_id == validated_mirror_id:
                mirror.sync_status = min(1.0, mirror.sync_status * 1.1)
            else:
                mirror.sync_status *= max(0.5, similarities[i])

            if mirror.sync_status < 0.5:
                await self._resync_mirror(mirror, validated_mirror_id)

    async def _resync_mirror(
        self,
        mirror: ProcessingMirror,
        reference_mirror_id: str
    ):
        try:
            reference_mirror = self.mirrors[reference_mirror_id]
            
            # Copy engine state
            mirror.engine = await self._clone_engine(reference_mirror.engine)
            
            # Clear caches
            mirror.results_cache.clear()
            mirror.active_tasks.clear()
            
            # Reset status
            mirror.sync_status = 0.8
            mirror.last_sync = datetime.now()
            
            logger.info(f"Resynced mirror {mirror.mirror_id}")
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Mirror resync error: {str(e)}")

    async def _clone_engine(
        self,
        source_engine: KaleidoscopeEngine
    ) -> KaleidoscopeEngine:
        """Creates a deep copy of an engine instance."""
        engine = KaleidoscopeEngine(
            num_logic_gears=len(source_engine.logic_gears),
            memory_graph=self.memory_graph
        )
        
        # Copy engine state
        engine.state = source_engine.state
        
        # Clone nodes
        for node_id, node in source_engine.nodes.items():
            engine.nodes[node_id] = await self._clone_node(node)
            
        return engine

    async def _clone_node(self, node: PureNode) -> PureNode:
        """Creates a deep copy of a node."""
        return PureNode(
            node_id=node.node_id,
            initial_dna=node.dna,
            entity_type=node.entity_type,
            parent_node=node.parent_node
        )

    async def _handle_validation_failure(
        self,
        results: List[Tuple[str, Dict[str, Any]]]
    ) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        try:
            # Attempt to reconcile results
            reconciled_result = await self._reconcile_results(results)
            if reconciled_result:
                return reconciled_result

            # If reconciliation fails, use highest confidence result
            confidence_scores = []
            for _, result in results:
                confidence = self._calculate_result_confidence(result)
                confidence_scores.append(confidence)

            if confidence_scores:
                best_idx = np.argmax(confidence_scores)
                return results[best_idx][1]

            return None

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Validation failure handling error: {str(e)}")
            return None

    def _calculate_result_confidence(self, result: Dict[str, Any]) -> float:
        try:
            confidence_factors = []
            
            # Check processed data
            if "processed_data" in result:
                data_confidence = self._evaluate_data_confidence(
                    result["processed_data"]
                )
                confidence_factors.append(data_confidence)

            # Check insights
            if "insights" in result:
                insight_confidence = self._evaluate_insight_confidence(
                    result["insights"]
                )
                confidence_factors.append(insight_confidence)

            # Check metrics
            if "metrics" in result:
                metric_confidence = self._evaluate_metric_confidence(
                    result["metrics"]
                )
                confidence_factors.append(metric_confidence)

            return np.mean(confidence_factors) if confidence_factors else 0.0

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Confidence calculation error: {str(e)}")
            return 0.0
# MirroredEngineValidation.py

# Mirrored Engine
class MirroredEngine:
    def __init__(self):
        self.memory_bank = []

    def validate_insights(self, insights):
        """Validates insights based on ethical and logical guidelines."""
        validated = []
        for insight in insights:
            if self._passes_guidelines(insight):
                validated.append({"insight": insight, "status": "approved"})
            else:
                validated.append({"insight": insight, "status": "rejected"})
        return validated

    def generate_alternative_perspectives(self, insights):
        """Creates speculative perspectives for validated insights."""
        alternatives = []
        for insight in insights:
            alternative = self._speculate_on_insight(insight)
            alternatives.append(alternative)
        return alternatives

    def _passes_guidelines(self, insight):
        """Placeholder for ethical and logical checks."""
        return True if "significance" in insight and insight["significance"] > 0.5 else False

    def _speculate_on_insight(self, insight):
        """Generate speculative insights by altering existing parameters."""
        speculative = {key: (value * 0.9 if isinstance(value, (int, float)) else value)
                       for key, value in insight.items()}
        speculative["type"] = f"speculative_{insight.get('type', 'unknown')}"
        return speculative

# Insight Validation System
class InsightValidator:
    def __init__(self):
        self.engine = MirroredEngine()

    def process_insights(self, insights):
        """Processes insights through validation and alternative perspective generation."""
        validated = self.engine.validate_insights(insights)
        speculative = self.engine.generate_alternative_perspectives([v["insight"] for v in validated if v["status"] == "approved"])
        return {
            "validated": validated,
            "speculative": speculative
        }

# Example Usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Sample insights
    sample_insights = [
        {"type": "prediction", "significance": 0.8, "details": "High confidence result"},
        {"type": "analysis", "significance": 0.4, "details": "Low confidence result"}
    ]

    # Process insights
    validator = InsightValidator()
    results = validator.process_insights(sample_insights)

    # Display results
    print("\n=== Validated Insights ===")
    for validated in results["validated"]:
        print(validated)

    print("\n=== Speculative Perspectives ===")
    for speculative in results["speculative"]:
        print(speculative)

# MirroredNetwork.py


class MirroredNetwork:
    def __init__(self):
        self.primary_network = {}
        self.secondary_network = {}
        self.feedback_loop_enabled = True

    def initialize_networks(self, nodes):
        """
        Initialize mirrored networks with the same structure.
        :param nodes: List of nodes to initialize.
        """
        print("Initializing mirrored networks...")
        for node in nodes:
            self.primary_network[node["name"]] = {"status": node["status"], "data": {}}
            self.secondary_network[node["name"]] = {"status": node["status"], "data": {}}
        print("Mirrored networks initialized.")

    def sync_networks(self):
        """
        Synchronize data between the primary and secondary networks.
        """
        print("Synchronizing mirrored networks...")
        for node_name in self.primary_network:
            if self.feedback_loop_enabled:
                # Example feedback mechanism: Average data across networks
                primary_data = self.primary_network[node_name]["data"]
                secondary_data = self.secondary_network[node_name]["data"]
                combined_data = {key: (primary_data.get(key, 0) + secondary_data.get(key, 0)) / 2
                                 for key in set(primary_data) | set(secondary_data)}
                self.primary_network[node_name]["data"] = combined_data
                self.secondary_network[node_name]["data"] = combined_data
        print("Mirrored networks synchronized.")

# Example usage of MirroredNetwork
if __name__ == "__main__":
    nodes = [{"name": "TextProcessor", "status": "active"},
             {"name": "VisualProcessor", "status": "idle"}]
    
    mirrored_network = MirroredNetwork()
    mirrored_network.initialize_networks(nodes)
    mirrored_network.sync_networks()

# mirrored_system.py

from node import Node

class MirroredSystem:
    def __init__(self, node1, node2):
        self.node1 = node1
        self.node2 = node2
        self.shared_knowledge = {}

    def perform_mirrored_learning(self, task1, task2):
        """Nodes collaborate on related tasks."""
        self.node1.learn(task1)
        self.node2.learn(task2)

        # Combine knowledge
        self.shared_knowledge.update(self.node1.knowledge)
        self.shared_knowledge.update(self.node2.knowledge)

        # Synchronize knowledge
        self.node1.knowledge.update(self.shared_knowledge)
        self.node2.knowledge.update(self.shared_knowledge)

    def replicate_mirrored_nodes(self, mutation_rate=0.1):
        """Replicate both nodes in the mirrored system."""
        child1 = self.node1.replicate(mutation_rate)
        child2 = self.node2.replicate(mutation_rate)
        return MirroredSystem(child1, child2) if child1 and child2 else None


# neural_resonance_coupler.py

# neural_resonance_coupler.py

class NeuralResonanceCoupler:
    def __init__(self, dimensions: int = 512):
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.coupling_matrix = np.zeros((dimensions, dimensions), dtype=np.complex128)
        self.resonance_fields = np.zeros((dimensions, 360), dtype=np.complex128)
        self.phase_memory = {}
        
    async def couple_resonances(self, 
                              neural_state: np.ndarray,
                              quantum_state: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """Couple neural and quantum resonance patterns"""
        # Calculate resonance fields
        neural_fields = self._calculate_neural_fields(neural_state)
        quantum_fields = self._calculate_quantum_fields(quantum_state)
        
        # Find resonance matches
        matches = self._find_resonance_matches(
            neural_fields,
            quantum_fields
        )
        
        # Optimize coupling
        optimized_coupling = self._optimize_coupling(
            matches,
            neural_state,
            quantum_state
        )
        
        # Generate coupled state
        coupled_state = self._generate_coupled_state(
            neural_state,
            quantum_state,
            optimized_coupling
        )
        
        return {
            'coupled_state': coupled_state,
            'coupling_strength': self._calculate_coupling_strength(
                optimized_coupling
            ),
            'resonance_matches': matches,
            'phase_coherence': self._calculate_phase_coherence(
                coupled_state
            )
        }
        
    def _calculate_neural_fields(self, state: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Calculate neural resonance fields"""
        fields = np.zeros((self.dimensions, 360), dtype=np.complex128)
        
        for i in range(self.dimensions):
            for angle in range(360):
                phase = angle * np.pi / 180
                
                # Calculate field with neural phase memory
                memory_phase = self.phase_memory.
# neural_resonance_coupler.py (continued)

    def _calculate_neural_fields(self, state: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Calculate neural resonance fields"""
        fields = np.zeros((self.dimensions, 360), dtype=np.complex128)
        
        for i in range(self.dimensions):
            for angle in range(360):
                phase = angle * np.pi / 180
                
                # Calculate field with neural phase memory
                memory_phase = self.phase_memory.get(i, 0.0)
                field_phase = phase + memory_phase
                
                # Generate resonance field
                fields[i, angle] = state[i] * np.exp(1j * field_phase)
                
                # Add quantum interference terms
                for j in range(self.dimensions):
                    if i != j:
                        coupling = self.coupling_matrix[i,j]
                        fields[i, angle] += (
                            0.1 * coupling * state[j] * 
                            np.exp(1j * (field_phase + np.angle(coupling)))
                        )
                        
        return fields

    def _find_resonance_matches(self,
                              neural_fields: np.ndarray,
                              quantum_fields: np.ndarray) -> List[Dict]:
        """Find matching resonance patterns between neural and quantum fields"""
        matches = []
        
        for i in range(self.dimensions):
            neural_field = neural_fields[i]
            
            for j in range(self.dimensions):
                quantum_field = quantum_fields[j]
                
                # Calculate field overlap
                overlap = np.abs(np.vdot(neural_field, quantum_field))
                
                if overlap > 0.7:  # Strong resonance threshold
                    # Calculate phase coherence
                    phase_diff = np.angle(
                        np.vdot(neural_field, quantum_field)
                    )
                    
                    # Calculate frequency matching
                    freq_match = self._calculate_frequency_match(
                        neural_field,
                        quantum_field
                    )
                    
                    matches.append({
                        'neural_dim': i,
                        'quantum_dim': j,
                        'overlap': float(overlap),
                        'phase_coherence': float(np.cos(phase_diff)),
                        'frequency_match': float(freq_match),
                        'coupling_strength': float(
                            overlap * np.cos(phase_diff) * freq_match
                        )
                    })
                    
        return matches

    def _optimize_coupling(self,
                         matches: List[Dict],
                         neural_state: np.ndarray,
                         quantum_state: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Optimize coupling between matched resonances"""
        coupling = np.zeros((self.dimensions, self.dimensions), dtype=np.complex128)
        
        for match in matches:
            i, j = match['neural_dim'], match['quantum_dim']
            
            # Calculate optimal coupling phase
            phase = self._calculate_optimal_phase(
                neural_state[i],
                quantum_state[j],
                match
            )
            
            # Set coupling strength
            coupling[i,j] = match['coupling_strength'] * np.exp(1j * phase)
            
            # Add symmetric coupling
            coupling[j,i] = np.conj(coupling[i,j])
            
        return coupling

    def _calculate_optimal_phase(self,
                               neural_state: complex,
                               quantum_state: complex,
                               match: Dict) -> float:
        """Calculate optimal coupling phase"""
        # Base phase from states
        state_phase = np.angle(neural_state) - np.angle(quantum_state)
        
        # Add resonance contribution
        resonance_phase = np.arctan2(
            match['frequency_match'],
            match['phase_coherence']
        )
        
        # Add quantum memory effect
        memory_phase = np.angle(
            self.phase_memory.get(match['neural_dim'], 1.0)
        )
        
        # Combine phases with weights
        optimal_phase = (
            0.5 * state_phase +
            0.3 * resonance_phase +
            0.2 * memory_phase
        )
        
        return optimal_phase

    def _generate_coupled_state(self,
                              neural_state: np.ndarray,
                              quantum_state: np.ndarray,
                              coupling: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Generate coupled quantum-neural state"""
        coupled_state = np.zeros(self.dimensions, dtype=np.complex128)
        
        for i in range(self.dimensions):
            # Direct state contribution
            coupled_state[i] = (
                0.7 * neural_state[i] +
                0.3 * quantum_state[i]
            )
            
            # Add coupling contributions
            for j in range(self.dimensions):
                if i != j:
                    coupled_state[i] += (
                        coupling[i,j] * quantum_state[j] +
                        coupling[j,i] * neural_state[j]
                    )
                    
        # Normalize state
        coupled_state /= np.linalg.norm(coupled_state)
        
        return coupled_state

"""Quantum mechanical operators.

from typing import Optional

from sympy.core.add import Add
from sympy.core.expr import Expr
from sympy.core.function import (Derivative, expand)
from sympy.core.mul import Mul
from sympy.core.numbers import oo
from sympy.core.singleton import S
from sympy.printing.pretty.stringpict import prettyForm
from sympy.physics.quantum.dagger import Dagger
from sympy.physics.quantum.qexpr import QExpr, dispatch_method

#-----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Operators and outer products
#-----------------------------------------------------------------------------


class Operator(QExpr):
    """Base class for non-commuting quantum operators.

    An operator maps between quantum states [1]_. In quantum mechanics,
    observables (including, but not limited to, measured physical values) are
    represented as Hermitian operators [2]_.

    Parameters
    ==========

    args : tuple
        The list of numbers or parameters that uniquely specify the
        operator. For time-dependent operators, this will include the time.

    Examples
    ========

    Create an operator and examine its attributes::

        >>> from sympy.physics.quantum import Operator
        >>> from sympy import I
        >>> A = Operator('A')
        >>> A
        A
        >>> A.hilbert_space
        H
        >>> A.label
        (A,)
        >>> A.is_commutative
        False

    Create another operator and do some arithmetic operations::

        >>> B = Operator('B')
        >>> C = 2*A*A + I*B
        >>> C
        2*A**2 + I*B

    Operators do not commute::

        >>> A.is_commutative
        False
        >>> B.is_commutative
        False
        >>> A*B == B*A
        False

    Polymonials of operators respect the commutation properties::

        >>> e = (A+B)**3
        >>> e.expand()
        A*B*A + A*B**2 + A**2*B + A**3 + B*A*B + B*A**2 + B**2*A + B**3

    Operator inverses are handle symbolically::

        >>> A.inv()
        A**(-1)
        >>> A*A.inv()
        1

    References
    ==========

    .. [1] https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Operator_%28physics%29
    .. [2] https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Observable
    """
    is_hermitian: Optional[bool] = None
    is_unitary: Optional[bool] = None
    @classmethod
    def default_args(self):
        return ("O",)

    #-------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Printing
    #-------------------------------------------------------------------------

    _label_separator = ','

    def _print_operator_name(self, printer, *args):
        return self.__class__.__name__

    _print_operator_name_latex = _print_operator_name

    def _print_operator_name_pretty(self, printer, *args):
        return prettyForm(self.__class__.__name__)

    def _print_contents(self, printer, *args):
        if len(self.label) == 1:
            return self._print_label(printer, *args)
        else:
            return '%s(%s)' % (
                self._print_operator_name(printer, *args),
                self._print_label(printer, *args)
            )

    def _print_contents_pretty(self, printer, *args):
        if len(self.label) == 1:
            return self._print_label_pretty(printer, *args)
        else:
            pform = self._print_operator_name_pretty(printer, *args)
            label_pform = self._print_label_pretty(printer, *args)
            label_pform = prettyForm(
                *label_pform.parens(left='(', right=')')
            )
            pform = prettyForm(*pform.right(label_pform))
            return pform

    def _print_contents_latex(self, printer, *args):
        if len(self.label) == 1:
            return self._print_label_latex(printer, *args)
        else:
            return r'%s\left(%s\right)' % (
                self._print_operator_name_latex(printer, *args),
                self._print_label_latex(printer, *args)
            )

    #-------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # _eval_* methods
    #-------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _eval_commutator(self, other, **options):
        """Evaluate [self, other] if known, return None if not known."""
        return dispatch_method(self, '_eval_commutator', other, **options)

    def _eval_anticommutator(self, other, **options):
        """Evaluate [self, other] if known."""
        return dispatch_method(self, '_eval_anticommutator', other, **options)

    #-------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Operator application
    #-------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _apply_operator(self, ket, **options):
        return dispatch_method(self, '_apply_operator', ket, **options)

    def _apply_from_right_to(self, bra, **options):
        return None

    def matrix_element(self, *args):
        raise NotImplementedError('matrix_elements is not defined')

    def inverse(self):
        return self._eval_inverse()

    inv = inverse

    def _eval_inverse(self):
        return self**(-1)

    def __mul__(self, other):

        if isinstance(other, IdentityOperator):
            return self

        return Mul(self, other)


class HermitianOperator(Operator):
    """A Hermitian operator that satisfies H == Dagger(H).

    Parameters
    ==========

    args : tuple
        The list of numbers or parameters that uniquely specify the
        operator. For time-dependent operators, this will include the time.

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy.physics.quantum import Dagger, HermitianOperator
    >>> H = HermitianOperator('H')
    >>> Dagger(H)
    H
    """

    is_hermitian = True

    def _eval_inverse(self):
        if isinstance(self, UnitaryOperator):
            return self
        else:
            return Operator._eval_inverse(self)

    def _eval_power(self, exp):
        if isinstance(self, UnitaryOperator):
            # so all eigenvalues of self are 1 or -1
            if exp.is_even:
                from sympy.core.singleton import S
                return S.One # is identity, see Issue 24153.
            elif exp.is_odd:
                return self
        # No simplification in all other cases
        return Operator._eval_power(self, exp)


class UnitaryOperator(Operator):
    """A unitary operator that satisfies U*Dagger(U) == 1.

    Parameters
    ==========

    args : tuple
        The list of numbers or parameters that uniquely specify the
        operator. For time-dependent operators, this will include the time.

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy.physics.quantum import Dagger, UnitaryOperator
    >>> U = UnitaryOperator('U')
    >>> U*Dagger(U)
    1
    """
    is_unitary = True
    def _eval_adjoint(self):
        return self._eval_inverse()


class IdentityOperator(Operator):
    """An identity operator I that satisfies op * I == I * op == op for any
    operator op.

    Parameters
    ==========

    N : Integer
        Optional parameter that specifies the dimension of the Hilbert space
        of operator. This is used when generating a matrix representation.

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy.physics.quantum import IdentityOperator
    >>> IdentityOperator()
    I
    """
    is_hermitian = True
    is_unitary = True
    @property
    def dimension(self):
        return self.N

    @classmethod
    def default_args(self):
        return (oo,)

    def __init__(self, *args, **hints):
        if not len(args) in (0, 1):
            raise ValueError('0 or 1 parameters expected, got %s' % args)

        self.N = args[0] if (len(args) == 1 and args[0]) else oo

    def _eval_commutator(self, other, **hints):
        return S.Zero

    def _eval_anticommutator(self, other, **hints):
        return 2 * other

    def _eval_inverse(self):
        return self

    def _eval_adjoint(self):
        return self

    def _apply_operator(self, ket, **options):
        return ket

    def _apply_from_right_to(self, bra, **options):
        return bra

    def _eval_power(self, exp):
        return self

    def _print_contents(self, printer, *args):
        return 'I'

    def _print_contents_pretty(self, printer, *args):
        return prettyForm('I')

    def _print_contents_latex(self, printer, *args):
        return r'{\mathcal{I}}'

    def __mul__(self, other):

        if isinstance(other, (Operator, Dagger)):
            return other

        return Mul(self, other)

    def _represent_default_basis(self, **options):
        if not self.N or self.N == oo:
            raise NotImplementedError('Cannot represent infinite dimensional' +
                                      ' identity operator as a matrix')

        format = options.get('format', 'sympy')
        if format != 'sympy':
            raise NotImplementedError('Representation in format ' +
                                      '%s not implemented.' % format)

        return eye(self.N)


class OuterProduct(Operator):
    """An unevaluated outer product between a ket and bra.

    This constructs an outer product between any subclass of ``KetBase`` and
    ``BraBase`` as ``|a><b|``. An ``OuterProduct`` inherits from Operator as they act as
    operators in quantum expressions.  For reference see [1]_.

    Parameters
    ==========

    ket : KetBase
        The ket on the left side of the outer product.
    bar : BraBase
        The bra on the right side of the outer product.

    Examples
    ========

    Create a simple outer product by hand and take its dagger::

        >>> from sympy.physics.quantum import Ket, Bra, OuterProduct, Dagger
        >>> from sympy.physics.quantum import Operator

        >>> k = Ket('k')
        >>> b = Bra('b')
        >>> op = OuterProduct(k, b)
        >>> op
        |k><b|
        >>> op.hilbert_space
        H
        >>> op.ket
        |k>
        >>> op.bra
        <b|
        >>> Dagger(op)
        |b><k|

    In simple products of kets and bras outer products will be automatically
    identified and created::

        >>> k*b
        |k><b|

    But in more complex expressions, outer products are not automatically
    created::

        >>> A = Operator('A')
        >>> A*k*b
        A*|k>*<b|

    A user can force the creation of an outer product in a complex expression
    by using parentheses to group the ket and bra::

        >>> A*(k*b)
        A*|k><b|

    References
    ==========

    .. [1] https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Outer_product
    """
    is_commutative = False

    def __new__(cls, *args, **old_assumptions):
        from sympy.physics.quantum.state import KetBase, BraBase

        if len(args) != 2:
            raise ValueError('2 parameters expected, got %d' % len(args))

        ket_expr = expand(args[0])
        bra_expr = expand(args[1])

        if (isinstance(ket_expr, (KetBase, Mul)) and
                isinstance(bra_expr, (BraBase, Mul))):
            ket_c, kets = ket_expr.args_cnc()
            bra_c, bras = bra_expr.args_cnc()

            if len(kets) != 1 or not isinstance(kets[0], KetBase):
                raise TypeError('KetBase subclass expected'
                                ', got: %r' % Mul(*kets))

            if len(bras) != 1 or not isinstance(bras[0], BraBase):
                raise TypeError('BraBase subclass expected'
                                ', got: %r' % Mul(*bras))

            if not kets[0].dual_class() == bras[0].__class__:
                raise TypeError(
                    'ket and bra are not dual classes: %r, %r' %
                    (kets[0].__class__, bras[0].__class__)
                    )

            # TODO: make sure the hilbert spaces of the bra and ket are
            # compatible
            obj = Expr.__new__(cls, *(kets[0], bras[0]), **old_assumptions)
            obj.hilbert_space = kets[0].hilbert_space
            return Mul(*(ket_c + bra_c)) * obj

        op_terms = []
        if isinstance(ket_expr, Add) and isinstance(bra_expr, Add):
            for ket_term in ket_expr.args:
                for bra_term in bra_expr.args:
                    op_terms.append(OuterProduct(ket_term, bra_term,
                                                 **old_assumptions))
        elif isinstance(ket_expr, Add):
            for ket_term in ket_expr.args:
                op_terms.append(OuterProduct(ket_term, bra_expr,
                                             **old_assumptions))
        elif isinstance(bra_expr, Add):
            for bra_term in bra_expr.args:
                op_terms.append(OuterProduct(ket_expr, bra_term,
                                             **old_assumptions))
        else:
            raise TypeError(
                'Expected ket and bra expression, got: %r, %r' %
                (ket_expr, bra_expr)
                )

        return Add(*op_terms)

    @property
    def ket(self):
        """Return the ket on the left side of the outer product."""
        return self.args[0]

    @property
    def bra(self):
