        """Return the bra on the right side of the outer product."""
        return self.args[1]

    def _eval_adjoint(self):
        return OuterProduct(Dagger(self.bra), Dagger(self.ket))

    def _sympystr(self, printer, *args):
        return printer._print(self.ket) + printer._print(self.bra)

    def _sympyrepr(self, printer, *args):
        return '%s(%s,%s)' % (self.__class__.__name__,
            printer._print(self.ket, *args), printer._print(self.bra, *args))

    def _pretty(self, printer, *args):
        pform = self.ket._pretty(printer, *args)
        return prettyForm(*pform.right(self.bra._pretty(printer, *args)))

    def _latex(self, printer, *args):
        k = printer._print(self.ket, *args)
        b = printer._print(self.bra, *args)
        return k + b

    def _represent(self, **options):
        k = self.ket._represent(**options)
        b = self.bra._represent(**options)
        return k*b

    def _eval_trace(self, **kwargs):
        # TODO if operands are tensorproducts this may be will be handled
        # differently.

        return self.ket._eval_trace(self.bra, **kwargs)


class DifferentialOperator(Operator):
    """An operator for representing the differential operator, i.e. d/dx

    It is initialized by passing two arguments. The first is an arbitrary
    expression that involves a function, such as ``Derivative(f(x), x)``. The
    second is the function (e.g. ``f(x)``) which we are to replace with the
    ``Wavefunction`` that this ``DifferentialOperator`` is applied to.

    Parameters
    ==========

    expr : Expr
           The arbitrary expression which the appropriate Wavefunction is to be
           substituted into

    func : Expr
           A function (e.g. f(x)) which is to be replaced with the appropriate
           Wavefunction when this DifferentialOperator is applied

    Examples
    ========

    You can define a completely arbitrary expression and specify where the
    Wavefunction is to be substituted

    >>> from sympy import Derivative, Function, Symbol
    >>> from sympy.physics.quantum.operator import DifferentialOperator
    >>> from sympy.physics.quantum.state import Wavefunction
    >>> from sympy.physics.quantum.qapply import qapply
    >>> f = Function('f')
    >>> x = Symbol('x')
    >>> d = DifferentialOperator(1/x*Derivative(f(x), x), f(x))
    >>> w = Wavefunction(x**2, x)
    >>> d.function
    f(x)
    >>> d.variables
    (x,)
    >>> qapply(d*w)
    Wavefunction(2, x)

    """

    @property
    def variables(self):
        """
        Returns the variables with which the function in the specified
        arbitrary expression is evaluated

        Examples
        ========

        >>> from sympy.physics.quantum.operator import DifferentialOperator
        >>> from sympy import Symbol, Function, Derivative
        >>> x = Symbol('x')
        >>> f = Function('f')
        >>> d = DifferentialOperator(1/x*Derivative(f(x), x), f(x))
        >>> d.variables
        (x,)
        >>> y = Symbol('y')
        >>> d = DifferentialOperator(Derivative(f(x, y), x) +
        ...                          Derivative(f(x, y), y), f(x, y))
        >>> d.variables
        (x, y)
        """

        return self.args[-1].args

    @property
    def function(self):
        """
        Returns the function which is to be replaced with the Wavefunction

        Examples
        ========

        >>> from sympy.physics.quantum.operator import DifferentialOperator
        >>> from sympy import Function, Symbol, Derivative
        >>> x = Symbol('x')
        >>> f = Function('f')
        >>> d = DifferentialOperator(Derivative(f(x), x), f(x))
        >>> d.function
        f(x)
        >>> y = Symbol('y')
        >>> d = DifferentialOperator(Derivative(f(x, y), x) +
        ...                          Derivative(f(x, y), y), f(x, y))
        >>> d.function
        f(x, y)
        """

        return self.args[-1]

    @property
    def expr(self):
        """
        Returns the arbitrary expression which is to have the Wavefunction
        substituted into it

        Examples
        ========

        >>> from sympy.physics.quantum.operator import DifferentialOperator
        >>> from sympy import Function, Symbol, Derivative
        >>> x = Symbol('x')
        >>> f = Function('f')
        >>> d = DifferentialOperator(Derivative(f(x), x), f(x))
        >>> d.expr
        Derivative(f(x), x)
        >>> y = Symbol('y')
        >>> d = DifferentialOperator(Derivative(f(x, y), x) +
        ...                          Derivative(f(x, y), y), f(x, y))
        >>> d.expr
        Derivative(f(x, y), x) + Derivative(f(x, y), y)
        """

        return self.args[0]

    @property
    def free_symbols(self):
        """
        Return the free symbols of the expression.
        """

        return self.expr.free_symbols

    def _apply_operator_Wavefunction(self, func, **options):
        from sympy.physics.quantum.state import Wavefunction
        var = self.variables
        wf_vars = func.args[1:]

        f = self.function
        new_expr = self.expr.subs(f, func(*var))
        new_expr = new_expr.doit()

        return Wavefunction(new_expr, *wf_vars)

    def _eval_derivative(self, symbol):
        new_expr = Derivative(self.expr, symbol)
        return DifferentialOperator(new_expr, self.args[-1])

    #-------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Printing
    #-------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _print(self, printer, *args):
        return '%s(%s)' % (
            self._print_operator_name(printer, *args),
            self._print_label(printer, *args)
        )

    def _print_pretty(self, printer, *args):
        pform = self._print_operator_name_pretty(printer, *args)
        label_pform = self._print_label_pretty(printer, *args)
        label_pform = prettyForm(
            *label_pform.parens(left='(', right=')')
        )
        pform = prettyForm(*pform.right(label_pform))
        return pform

# parallel-processor.py

from __future__ import annotations
import asyncio
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Set, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import logging
from datetime import datetime
import numpy as np
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, ProcessPoolExecutor
import uuid

from ..core.nodes.pure_node import PureNode
from ..core.engine.kaleidoscope_engine import KaleidoscopeEngine
from ..memory.bank import MemoryBank
from ..utils.validation import validate_processing_output

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

@dataclass
class ProcessingStream:
    """Represents a parallel processing stream with its own resources."""
    stream_id: str
    processor_count: int
    memory_allocation: float  # GB
    active_tasks: int = 0
    processing_history: List[Dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    stream_health: float = 1.0
    last_sync: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)

class ParallelProcessor:
    """
    Manages parallel processing streams and synchronization for the Kaleidoscope AI system.
    Implements advanced parallel processing with self-validation and error correction.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        num_streams: int = 3,
        processors_per_stream: int = 4,
        memory_per_stream: float = 2.0,  # GB
        validation_threshold: float = 0.8
    ):
        self.streams: Dict[str, ProcessingStream] = {}
        self.executors: Dict[str, ProcessPoolExecutor] = {}
        self.thread_pool = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_streams * 2)
        
        # Initialize processing streams
        for _ in range(num_streams):
            self._create_stream(processors_per_stream, memory_per_stream)
            
        self.validation_threshold = validation_threshold
        self.synchronization_queue = asyncio.Queue()
        self.result_cache: Dict[str, List[Any]] = {}
        
        # Performance monitoring
        self.performance_metrics = {
            "successful_syncs": 0,
            "validation_failures": 0,
            "error_corrections": 0,
            "processing_time": []
        }
        
        logger.info(f"Parallel processor initialized with {num_streams} streams")

    def _create_stream(self, processor_count: int, memory_allocation: float) -> str:
        """Creates a new processing stream."""
        stream_id = f"stream_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
        stream = ProcessingStream(
            stream_id=stream_id,
            processor_count=processor_count,
            memory_allocation=memory_allocation
        )
        
        self.streams[stream_id] = stream
        self.executors[stream_id] = ProcessPoolExecutor(
            max_workers=processor_count
        )
        
        return stream_id

    async def process_parallel(
        self,
        task: Dict[str, Any],
        validation_required: bool = True
    ) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Processes a task across multiple streams with validation.
        
        Args:
            task: Task to process
            validation_required: Whether to validate results
            
        Returns:
            Processed and validated result
        """
        start_time = datetime.now()
        processing_futures = []
        
        # Submit task to all streams
        for stream_id, executor in self.executors.items():
            if self.streams[stream_id].stream_health > 0.5:
                future = self.thread_pool.submit(
                    self._process_in_stream,
                    executor,
                    task,
                    stream_id
                )
                processing_futures.append(future)
                self.streams[stream_id].active_tasks += 1

        try:
            # Gather results from all streams
            results = []
            for future in processing_futures:
                try:
                    result = await asyncio.wrap_future(future)
                    if result:
                        results.append(result)
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.error(f"Stream processing error: {str(e)}")

            if not results:
                return None

            # Validate and synchronize results
            if validation_required:
                final_result = await self._validate_and_sync_results(results)
            else:
                final_result = results[0]

            # Update performance metrics
            processing_time = (datetime.now() - start_time).total_seconds()
            self.performance_metrics["processing_time"].append(processing_time)

            return final_result

        finally:
            # Cleanup
            for stream_id in self.streams:
                self.streams[stream_id].active_tasks -= 1

    def _process_in_stream(
        self,
        executor: ProcessPoolExecutor,
        task: Dict[str, Any],
        stream_id: str
    ) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Processes a task within a specific stream."""
        try:
            # Execute task in process pool
            result = executor.submit(
                self._execute_task,
                task
            ).result()

            if result:
                # Record processing history
                self.streams[stream_id].processing_history.append({
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
                    "task_id": task.get("id", "unknown"),
                    "success": True
                })
                
                return result
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Stream {stream_id} processing error: {str(e)}")
            self.streams[stream_id].stream_health *= 0.9
            
        return None

    @staticmethod
    def _execute_task(task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Executes a task in a separate process."""
        try:
            # Task execution logic here
            # This would integrate with the KaleidoscopeEngine
            result = {
                "task_id": task.get("id", "unknown"),
                "status": "completed",
                "result": task.get("data", {})  # Replace with actual processing
            }
            
            return result
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Task execution error: {str(e)}")
            return None

    async def _validate_and_sync_results(
        self,
        results: List[Dict[str, Any]]
    ) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Validates and synchronizes results from multiple streams.
        
        Args:
            results: List of results from different streams
            
        Returns:
            Validated and synchronized result
        """
        if not results:
            return None

        # Compare results for consistency
        consistency_scores = []
        for i, result1 in enumerate(results):
            score = 0
            for j, result2 in enumerate(results):
                if i != j:
                    score += self._calculate_result_similarity(result1, result2)
            consistency_scores.append(score / (len(results) - 1))

        # Check if results are consistent enough
        if max(consistency_scores) >= self.validation_threshold:
            # Use result with highest consistency score
            best_result_idx = consistency_scores.index(max(consistency_scores))
            self.performance_metrics["successful_syncs"] += 1
            return results[best_result_idx]
        else:
            # Handle validation failure
            self.performance_metrics["validation_failures"] += 1
            return await self._handle_validation_failure(results)

    def _calculate_result_similarity(
        self,
        result1: Dict[str, Any],
        result2: Dict[str, Any]
    ) -> float:
        """Calculates similarity between two results."""
        try:
            # Compare key elements
            if result1.get("task_id") != result2.get("task_id"):
                return 0.0

            # Compare actual results
            similarity = self._compare_nested_structures(
                result1.get("result", {}),
                result2.get("result", {})
            )
            
            return similarity
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Similarity calculation error: {str(e)}")
            return 0.0

    def _compare_nested_structures(
        self,
        struct1: Any,
        struct2: Any,
        depth: int = 0,
        max_depth: int = 5
    ) -> float:
        """Recursively compares nested data structures."""
        if depth >= max_depth:
            return 1.0 if struct1 == struct2 else 0.0

        if type(struct1) != type(struct2):
            return 0.0

        if isinstance(struct1, dict):
            if not struct1 or not struct2:
                return 1.0 if struct1 == struct2 else 0.0
                
            similarities = []
            for key in set(struct1.keys()) & set(struct2.keys()):
                similarity = self._compare_nested_structures(
                    struct1[key],
                    struct2[key],
                    depth + 1
                )
                similarities.append(similarity)
                
            return sum(similarities) / len(similarities) if similarities else 0.0
            
        elif isinstance(struct1, (list, tuple)):
            if not struct1 or not struct2:
                return 1.0 if struct1 == struct2 else 0.0
                
            similarities = []
            for item1, item2 in zip(struct1, struct2):
                similarity = self._compare_nested_structures(
                    item1,
                    item2,
                    depth + 1
                )
                similarities.append(similarity)
                
            return sum(similarities) / len(similarities) if similarities else 0.0
            
        elif isinstance(struct1, (int, float)):
            if struct1 == struct2:
                return 1.0
            # Compare numerical values with tolerance
            try:
                diff = abs(struct1 - struct2)
                max_val = max(abs(struct1), abs(struct2))
                if max_val == 0:
                    return 1.0 if diff == 0 else 0.0
                return max(0.0, 1.0 - (diff / max_val))
            except:
                return 0.0
                
        else:
            return 1.0 if struct1 == struct2 else 0.0

    async def _handle_validation_failure(
        self,
        results: List[Dict[str, Any]]
    ) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Handles cases where result validation fails."""
        try:
            # Attempt error correction
            corrected_result = await self._attempt_error_correction(results)
            if corrected_result:
                self.performance_metrics["error_corrections"] += 1
                return corrected_result

            # If correction fails, return most common result
            if len(results) >= 2:
                result_hashes = [hash(str(r)) for r in results]
                most_common = max(set(result_hashes), key=result_hashes.count)
                return next(r for r in results if hash(str(r)) == most_common)

            return results[0] if results else None

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Validation failure handling error: {str(e)}")
            return None

    async def _attempt_error_correction(
        self,
        results: List[Dict[str, Any]]
    ) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Attempts to correct errors in processing results."""
        try:
            # Identify common elements across results
            common_elements = self._identify_common_elements(results)
            
            # Create corrected result structure
            corrected_result = {
                "task_id": results[0].get("task_id", "unknown"),
                "status": "corrected",
                "result": common_elements,
                "correction_info": {
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
                    "num_source_results": len(results),
                    "correction_confidence": self._calculate_correction_confidence(
                        results,
                        common_elements
                    )
                }
            }
            
            return corrected_result
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error correction failed: {str(e)}")
            return None

    def _identify_common_elements(
        self,
        results: List[Dict[str, Any]]
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Identifies elements that are common across multiple results."""
        common_elements = {}
        
        if not results:
            return common_elements

        # Get all keys from all results
        all_keys = set()
        for result in results:
            all_keys.update(result.get("result", {}).keys())

        # Find common elements
        for key in all_keys:
            values = []
            for result in results:
                if key in result.get("result", {}):
                    values.append(result["result"][key])

            if values:
                # Use most common value or average for numbers
                if all(isinstance(v, (int, float)) for v in values):
                    common_elements[key] = sum(values) / len(values)
                else:
                    common_elements[key] = max(
                        set(values),
                        key=values.count
                    )

        return common_elements

    def _calculate_correction_confidence(
        self,
        results: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        corrected_elements: Dict[str, Any]
    ) -> float:
        """Calculates confidence in error correction."""
        try:
            confidences = []
            
            for result in results:
                similarity = self._compare_nested_structures(
                    result.get("result", {}),
                    corrected_elements
                )
                confidences.append(similarity)

            return sum(confidences) / len(confidences) if confidences else 0.0
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Confidence calculation error: {str(e)}")
            return 0.0

    def get_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Returns current status of the parallel processor."""
        return {
            "active_streams": len(self.streams),
            "total_active_tasks": sum(
                stream.active_tasks for stream in self.streams.values()
            ),
            "average_stream_health": np.mean([
                stream.stream_health for stream in self.streams.values()
            ]),
            "performance_metrics": {
                "successful_syncs": self.performance_metrics["successful_syncs"],
                "validation_failures": self.performance_metrics["validation_failures"],
                "error_corrections": self.performance_metrics["error_corrections"],
                "average_processing_time": np.mean(
                    self.performance_metrics["processing_time"]
                ) if self.performance_metrics["processing_time"] else 0.0
            }
        }
# pattern-analyzer.py

import numpy as np
import torch
import networkx as nx
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
from scipy.sparse.linalg import eigsh
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Set
from dataclasses import dataclass
import logging

@dataclass
class PatternState:
    """Pattern state with quantum properties"""
    pattern_id: str
    dimensions: List[int]
    strength: float
    coherence: float
    connections: Set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    evolution_history: List[Dict] = field(default_factory=list)

class QuantumPatternAnalyzer:
    def __init__(self, 
                embedding_dim: int = 768,
                num_patterns: int = 1000):
        self.embedding_dim = embedding_dim
        self.num_patterns = num_patterns
        self.pattern_memory = {}
        self.pattern_graph = nx.DiGraph()
        self.stability_threshold = 0.7
        
        # Initialize pattern embeddings
        self.pattern_embeddings = np.zeros((num_patterns, embedding_dim))
        self.pattern_quantum_states = np.zeros((num_patterns, embedding_dim), 
                                             dtype=np.complex128)
        
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

    def analyze_patterns(self, 
                        embedding: torch.Tensor,
                        quantum_state: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """Analyze neural and quantum patterns"""
        try:
            # Extract pattern features
            pattern_features = self._extract_pattern_features(
                embedding, quantum_state
            )
            
            # Detect significant patterns
            patterns = self._detect_patterns(pattern_features)
            
            # Update pattern memory and graph
            self._update_pattern_memory(patterns)
            
            # Find pattern relationships
            relationships = self._find_pattern_relationships(patterns)
            
            return {
                'patterns': patterns,
                'relationships': relationships,
                'memory_state': self._get_memory_state()
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Pattern analysis error: {str(e)}")
            raise

    def _extract_pattern_features(self,
                                embedding: torch.Tensor,
                                quantum_state: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Extract combined pattern features"""
        # Convert embedding to numpy
        emb_features = embedding.detach().numpy()
        
        # Calculate quantum features
        quantum_features = self._calculate_quantum_features(quantum_state)
        
        # Combine features
        combined = np.concatenate([
            emb_features,
            quantum_features
        ], axis=-1)
        
        return combined

    def _calculate_quantum_features(self, quantum_state: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Calculate quantum mechanical features"""
        features = []
        
        # Amplitude features
        amplitudes = np.abs(quantum_state)
        features.extend([
            np.mean(amplitudes),
            np.std(amplitudes),
            np.max(amplitudes),
            entropy(amplitudes)
        ])
        
        # Phase features
        phases = np.angle(quantum_state)
        features.extend([
            circular_mean(phases),
            circular_std(phases),
            phase_coherence(phases)
        ])
        
        # Entanglement features
        rho = np.outer(quantum_state, np.conjugate(quantum_state))
        features.extend([
            von_neumann_entropy(rho),
            participation_ratio(rho),
            quantum_discord(rho)
        ])
        
        return np.array(features)

    def _detect_patterns(self, features: np.ndarray) -> List[Dict]:
        """Detect significant patterns in feature space"""
        patterns = []
        
        # Calculate similarity with existing patterns
        similarities = np.dot(self.pattern_embeddings, features.T)
        similar_patterns = np.where(similarities > self.stability_threshold)[0]
        
        # Update existing patterns
        for idx in similar_patterns:
            pattern = self._update_pattern(idx, features)
            patterns.append(pattern)
            
        # Create new pattern if no similar patterns found
        if len(patterns) == 0:
            pattern = self._create_new_pattern(features)
            patterns.append(pattern)
            
        return patterns

    def _update_pattern(self, pattern_idx: int, features: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """Update existing pattern with new features"""
        pattern_id = f"pattern_{pattern_idx}"
        pattern = self.pattern_memory[pattern_id]
        
        # Calculate pattern similarity
        similarity = np.dot(self.pattern_embeddings[pattern_idx], features)
        
        # Update pattern embedding using exponential moving average
        alpha = 0.1
        self.pattern_embeddings[pattern_idx] = (1 - alpha) * self.pattern_embeddings[pattern_idx] + alpha * features
        
        # Update quantum state
        quantum_features = features[self.embedding_dim:]
        self.pattern_quantum_states[pattern_idx] = self._evolve_quantum_state(
            self.pattern_quantum_states[pattern_idx],
            quantum_features
        )
        
        # Update pattern metrics
        pattern.strength = float(similarity)
        pattern.coherence = self._calculate_coherence(self.pattern_quantum_states[pattern_idx])
        
        # Track evolution history
        pattern.evolution_history.append({
            'similarity': float(similarity),
            'coherence': pattern.coherence,
            'timestamp': time.time()
        })
        
        return {
            'id': pattern_id,
            'strength': pattern.strength,
            'coherence': pattern.coherence,
            'dimensions': pattern.dimensions,
            'stability': self._calculate_pattern_stability(pattern)
        }
        
    def _evolve_quantum_state(self, current_state: np.ndarray, features: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Evolve quantum state based on new features"""
        # Construct evolution operator
        H = np.zeros((len(current_state), len(current_state)), dtype=np.complex128)
        
        # Add kinetic terms
        for i in range(len(current_state)):
            H[i,i] = features[i % len(features)]
            if i > 0:
                H[i,i-1] = H[i-1,i] = 0.1j * features[i % len(features)]
                
        # Evolution operator
        U = linalg.expm(-1j * H * 0.1)
        
        # Evolve state
        new_state = U @ current_state
        
        # Normalize
        new_state /= np.linalg.norm(new_state)
        
        return new_state
        
    def _calculate_coherence(self, quantum_state: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """Calculate quantum coherence of state"""
        # Construct density matrix
        rho = np.outer(quantum_state, np.conjugate(quantum_state))
        
        # Calculate l1-norm coherence
        coherence = 0.0
        n = len(quantum_state)
        for i in range(n):
            for j in range(n):
                if i != j:
                    coherence += np.abs(rho[i,j])
        
        return float(coherence)
        
    def _calculate_pattern_stability(self, pattern: PatternState) -> float:
        """Calculate pattern stability from evolution history"""
        if len(pattern.evolution_history) < 2:
            return 1.0
            
        # Calculate stability metrics
        coherence_stability = np.std([e['coherence'] for e in pattern.evolution_history[-10:]])
        strength_stability = np.std([e['similarity'] for e in pattern.evolution_history[-10:]])
        
        # Combine metrics
        stability = np.exp(-(coherence_stability + strength_stability))
        return float(stability)
        
    def _create_new_pattern(self, features: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """Create new pattern from features"""
        # Find empty slot
        for idx in range(self.num_patterns):
            if f"pattern_{idx}" not in self.pattern_memory:
                pattern_idx = idx
                break
        else:
            # Replace oldest pattern if no empty slots
            pattern_idx = self._find_oldest_pattern()
            
        pattern_id = f"pattern_{pattern_idx}"
        
        # Initialize quantum state
        quantum_features = features[self.embedding_dim:]
        quantum_state = quantum_features / np.linalg.norm(quantum_features)
        
        # Store pattern
        self.pattern_embeddings[pattern_idx] = features
        self.pattern_quantum_states[pattern_idx] = quantum_state
        
        # Create pattern state
        pattern = PatternState(
            pattern_id=pattern_id,
            dimensions=list(range(len(quantum_state))),
            strength=1.0,
            coherence=self._calculate_coherence(quantum_state)
        )
        self.pattern_memory[pattern_id] = pattern
        
        return {
            'id': pattern_id,
            'strength': pattern.strength, 
            'coherence': pattern.coherence,
            'dimensions': pattern.dimensions,
            'stability': 1.0
        }
        
    def _find_pattern_relationships(self, patterns: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:
        """Find relationships between patterns"""
        relationships = []
        
        for i, p1 in enumerate(patterns):
            for p2 in patterns[i+1:]:
                # Calculate relationship strength
                quantum_overlap = self._calculate_quantum_overlap(
                    self.pattern_quantum_states[int(p1['id'].split('_')[1])],
                    self.pattern_quantum_states[int(p2['id'].split('_')[1])]
                )
                
                embedding_sim = np.dot(
                    self.pattern_embeddings[int(p1['id'].split('_')[1])],
                    self.pattern_embeddings[int(p2['id'].split('_')[1])]
                )
                
                relationship_strength = 0.5 * (quantum_overlap + embedding_sim)
                
                if relationship_strength > self.stability_threshold:
                    relationship = {
                        'patterns': [p1['id'], p2['id']],
                        'strength': float(relationship_strength),
                        'quantum_overlap': float(quantum_overlap),
                        'embedding_similarity': float(embedding_sim)
                    }
                    relationships.append(relationship)
                    
                    # Update pattern graph
                    self.pattern_graph.add_edge(
                        p1['id'], 
                        p2['id'],
                        weight=relationship_strength,
                        quantum_overlap=quantum_overlap,
                        embedding_sim=embedding_sim
                    )
        
        # Remove weak relationships
        self._prune_pattern_graph()
        
        return relationships
        
    def _calculate_quantum_overlap(self, state1: np.ndarray, state2: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """Calculate quantum state overlap"""
        return float(np.abs(np.dot(np.conjugate(state1), state2)))
        
    def _prune_pattern_graph(self):
        """Remove weak relationships from pattern graph"""
        for u, v, data in list(self.pattern_graph.edges(data=True)):
            if data['weight'] < self.stability_threshold:
                self.pattern_graph.remove_edge(u, v)
                
        # Remove isolated nodes
        for node in list(self.pattern_graph.nodes()):
            if self.pattern_graph.degree(node) == 0:
                self.pattern_graph.remove_node(node)
                
    def _get_memory_state(self) -> Dict:
        """Get current state of pattern memory"""
        # Calculate memory metrics
        active_patterns = len(self.pattern_memory)
        total_relationships = self.pattern_graph.number_of_edges()
        
        # Calculate average pattern metrics
        pattern_metrics = {
            'strength': np.mean([p.strength for p in self.pattern_memory.values()]),
            'coherence': np.mean([p.coherence for p in self.pattern_memory.values()]),
            'stability': np.mean([
                self._calculate_pattern_stability(p) for p in self.pattern_memory.values()
            ])
        }
        
        # Graph metrics
        graph_metrics = {
            'density': nx.density(self.pattern_graph),
            'clustering': nx.average_clustering(self.pattern_graph, weight='weight'),
            'avg_path_length': nx.average_shortest_path_length(
                self.pattern_graph, weight='weight'
            ) if nx.is_connected(self.pattern_graph) else float('inf')
        }
        
        # Quantum metrics
        quantum_metrics = {
            'avg_overlap': np.mean([
                data['quantum_overlap'] for _, _, data in self.pattern_graph.edges(data=True)
            ]),
            'max_overlap': np.max([
                data['quantum_overlap'] for _, _, data in self.pattern_graph.edges(data=True)
            ]) if self.pattern_graph.edges else 0.0
        }
        
        return {
            'active_patterns': active_patterns,
            'total_relationships': total_relationships,
            'pattern_metrics': pattern_metrics,
            'graph_metrics': graph_metrics,
            'quantum_metrics': quantum_metrics,
            'memory_usage': self._calculate_memory_usage()
        }
        
    def _calculate_memory_usage(self) -> Dict:
        """Calculate memory usage statistics"""
        embedding_size = self.pattern_embeddings.nbytes / (1024 * 1024)  # MB
        quantum_size = self.pattern_quantum_states.nbytes / (1024 * 1024)  # MB
        graph_size = len(self.pattern_graph.edges()) * 32 / (1024 * 1024)  # MB
        
        return {
            'embedding_memory': float(embedding_size),
            'quantum_memory': float(quantum_size),
            'graph_memory': float(graph_size),
            'total_memory': float(embedding_size + quantum_size + graph_size)
        }

    def _find_oldest_pattern(self) -> int:
        """Find index of oldest pattern based on last update"""
        oldest_time = float('inf')
        oldest_idx = 0
        
        for pattern_id, pattern in self.pattern_memory.items():
            if pattern.evolution_history:
                last_update = pattern.evolution_history[-1]['timestamp']
                if last_update < oldest_time:
                    oldest_time = last_update
                    oldest_idx = int(pattern_id.split('_')[1])
                    
        return oldest_idx
        
# pattern_annalys.py

import numpy as np
import logging

# Configure logging for PatternAnalysis
logging.basicConfig(
    filename="pattern_analysis.log",
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s"
)

class PatternAnalysis:
    @staticmethod
    def detect_trends(data):
        """
        Detects upward or downward trends in a numerical dataset.

        Args:
            data (list): List of numerical values.

        Returns:
            str: 'upward', 'downward', or 'no trend'.
        """
        try:
            if len(data) < 2:
                logging.warning("Insufficient data for trend detection.")
                return "no trend"

            diffs = np.diff(data)
            if np.all(diffs > 0):
                trend = "upward"
            elif np.all(diffs < 0):
                trend = "downward"
            else:
                trend = "no trend"

            logging.info(f"Trend detected: {trend}")
            return trend
        except Exception as e:
            logging.error(f"Error detecting trends: {e}")
            raise

    @staticmethod
    def find_anomalies(data, threshold=1.5):
        """
        Identifies anomalies in data using the interquartile range method.

        Args:
            data (list): List of numerical values.
            threshold (float): Threshold multiplier for determining outliers.

        Returns:
            list: Indices of anomalous values.
        """
        try:
            data = np.array(data)
            q1 = np.percentile(data, 25)
            q3 = np.percentile(data, 75)
            iqr = q3 - q1

            lower_bound = q1 - threshold * iqr
            upper_bound = q3 + threshold * iqr

            anomalies = np.where((data < lower_bound) | (data > upper_bound))[0]

            logging.info(f"Anomalies detected at indices: {anomalies.tolist()}")
            return anomalies.tolist()
        except Exception as e:
            logging.error(f"Error finding anomalies: {e}")
            raise

    @staticmethod
    def cluster_patterns(data, n_clusters=3):
        """
        Clusters data into groups using k-means clustering.

        Args:
            data (list): List of numerical values.
            n_clusters (int): Number of clusters.

        Returns:
            list: Cluster labels for each data point.
        """
        try:
            from sklearn.cluster import KMeans

            data = np.array(data).reshape(-1, 1)
            kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=n_clusters, random_state=42)
            kmeans.fit(data)

            labels = kmeans.labels_
            logging.info(f"Data clustered into {n_clusters} clusters: {labels.tolist()}")

            return labels.tolist()
        except Exception as e:
            logging.error(f"Error clustering patterns: {e}")
            raise

# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    analyzer = PatternAnalysis()

    # Trend detection
    data = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]
    print("Trend:", analyzer.detect_trends(data))

    # Anomaly detection
    data_with_anomalies = [10, 12, 15, 100, 18, 19]

    print("Anomalies:", analyzer.find_anomalies(data_with_anomalies))

    # Clustering patterns
    cluster_data = [1, 2, 2, 3, 10, 12, 12, 20]
    print("Clusters:", analyzer.cluster_patterns(cluster_data))

# pattern-processor(1).py

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
from scipy.sparse.linalg import eigsh
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Set
from dataclasses import dataclass
import logging

@dataclass
class PatternState:
    """Represents the state of a detected pattern"""
    pattern_id: str
    dimensions: List[int]
    strength: float
    coherence: float
    connections: Set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    evolution_history: List[Dict] = field(default_factory=list)

class AdvancedPatternProcessor:
    def __init__(self, quantum_core):
        self.quantum_core = quantum_core
        self.pattern_graph = nx.DiGraph()
        self.pattern_memory = {}
        self.stability_threshold = 0.7
        self.resonance_field = np.zeros((quantum_core.dimensions, 360), dtype=np.complex128)
        self._initialize_processor()

    def _initialize_processor(self):
        """Initialize the pattern processor with advanced detection capabilities"""
        # Create initial pattern detection fields
        for dim in range(self.quantum_core.dimensions):
            angle_space = np.linspace(0, 2*np.pi, 360)
            self.resonance_field[dim] = np.exp(1j * angle_space)

    def process_patterns(self, quantum_data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Process quantum data for advanced pattern detection"""
        resonances = quantum_data['resonance_patterns']
        transformed_data = quantum_data['transformed_data']
        
        # Detect temporal patterns
        temporal_patterns = self._detect_temporal_patterns(resonances)
        
        # Detect spatial patterns
        spatial_patterns = self._detect_spatial_patterns(transformed_data)
        
        # Synthesize higher-order patterns
        advanced_patterns = self._synthesize_patterns(temporal_patterns, spatial_patterns)
        
        # Update pattern graph
        self._update_pattern_graph(advanced_patterns)
        
        return {
            'temporal_patterns': temporal_patterns,
            'spatial_patterns': spatial_patterns,
            'advanced_patterns': advanced_patterns,
            'graph_metrics': self._calculate_graph_metrics()
        }

    def _detect_temporal_patterns(self, resonances: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:
        """Detect temporal evolution patterns in resonances"""
        temporal_patterns = []
        
        # Convert resonances to time series
        time_series = self._create_time_series(resonances)
        
        # Apply Singular Spectrum Analysis (SSA)
        window_size = min(len(time_series), 20)
        if window_size > 2:
            trajectory_matrix = self._create_trajectory_matrix(time_series, window_size)
            U, S, Vt = np.linalg.svd(trajectory_matrix)
            
            # Analyze principal components
            for i in range(min(3, len(S))):
                if S[i] > 0.1 * S[0]:  # Significant component threshold
                    pattern = {
                        'type': 'temporal',
                        'strength': float(S[i]),
                        'component': U[:, i].tolist(),
                        'frequency': self._estimate_frequency(U[:, i]),
                        'stability': self._calculate_stability(U[:, i])
                    }
                    temporal_patterns.append(pattern)
        
        return temporal_patterns

    def _detect_spatial_patterns(self, data: np.ndarray) -> List[Dict]:
        """Detect spatial patterns using spectral clustering"""
        spatial_patterns = []
        
        # Create similarity matrix
        similarity = np.abs(np.dot(data, data.conj().T))
        
        # Apply spectral clustering
        laplacian = self._create_normalized_laplacian(similarity)
        eigenvalues, eigenvectors = eigsh(laplacian, k=min(5, data.shape[0]-1), which='SM')
        
        # Analyze eigenvectors for spatial patterns
        for i, (eval, evec) in enumerate(zip(eigenvalues, eigenvectors.T)):
            if eval < 0.5:  # Strong spatial correlation threshold
                pattern = {
                    'type': 'spatial',
                    'strength': float(1 - eval),
                    'dimensions': self._get_pattern_dimensions(evec),
                    'coherence': self._calculate_spatial_coherence(evec),
                    'symmetry': self._analyze_symmetry(evec)
                }
                spatial_patterns.append(pattern)
        
        return spatial_patterns

    def _synthesize_patterns(self, temporal_patterns: List[Dict], spatial_patterns: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:
        """Synthesize higher-order patterns from temporal and spatial patterns"""
        advanced_patterns = []
        
        # Find correlations between temporal and spatial patterns
        for t_pattern in temporal_patterns:
            for s_pattern in spatial_patterns:
                correlation = self._calculate_pattern_correlation(t_pattern, s_pattern)
                if correlation > self.stability_threshold:
                    pattern_id = f"pattern_{len(self.pattern_memory)}"
                    advanced_pattern = PatternState(
                        pattern_id=pattern_id,
                        dimensions=s_pattern['dimensions'],
                        strength=np.mean([t_pattern['strength'], s_pattern['strength']]),
                        coherence=correlation
                    )
                    self.pattern_memory[pattern_id] = advanced_pattern
                    advanced_patterns.append({
                        'type': 'advanced',
                        'temporal_component': t_pattern,
                        'spatial_component': s_pattern,
                        'correlation': correlation,
                        'pattern_id': pattern_id
                    })
        
        return advanced_patterns

    def _update_pattern_graph(self, patterns: List[Dict]):
        """Update pattern graph with new patterns and their relationships"""
        # Add new patterns to graph
        for pattern in patterns:
            if pattern['pattern_id'] not in self.pattern_graph:
                self.pattern_graph.add_node(
                    pattern['pattern_id'],
                    type='advanced',
                    strength=pattern['spatial_component']['strength'],
                    coherence=pattern['correlation']
                )
        
        # Update edges based on pattern relationships
        for p1 in patterns:
            for p2 in patterns:
                if p1['pattern_id'] != p2['pattern_id']:
                    relationship = self._calculate_pattern_relationship(p1, p2)
                    if relationship > self.stability_threshold:
                        self.pattern_graph.add_edge(
                            p1['pattern_id'],
                            p2['pattern_id'],
                            weight=relationship
                        )

    def _calculate_graph_metrics(self) -> Dict:
        """Calculate advanced metrics for the pattern graph"""
        return {
            'density': nx.density(self.pattern_graph),
            'clustering': nx.average_clustering(self.pattern_graph),
            'centrality': self._calculate_pattern_centrality(),
            'stability': self._calculate_graph_stability()
        }

    def _calculate_pattern_relationship(self, p1: Dict, p2: Dict) -> float:
        """Calculate relationship strength between two patterns"""
        temporal_sim = self._calculate_temporal_similarity(
            p1['temporal_component'],
            p2['temporal_component']
        )
        spatial_sim = self._calculate_spatial_similarity(
            p1['spatial_component'],
            p2['spatial_component']
        )
        return np.mean([temporal_sim, spatial_sim])

    def _calculate_pattern_centrality(self) -> Dict:
        """Calculate centrality measures for patterns"""
        centrality = {}
        if len(self.pattern_graph) > 0:
            eigenvector_centrality = nx.eigenvector_centrality_numpy(self.pattern_graph)
            pagerank = nx.pagerank(self.pattern_graph)
            
            for pattern_id in self.pattern_graph.nodes():
                centrality[pattern_id] = {
                    'eigenvector': float(eigenvector_centrality[pattern_id]),
                    'pagerank': float(pagerank[pattern_id])
                }
        return centrality

    def _calculate_graph_stability(self) -> float:
        """Calculate overall stability of the pattern graph"""
        if len(self.pattern_graph) < 2:
            return 1.0
            
        # Calculate graph Laplacian eigenvalues
        laplacian = nx.normalized_laplacian_matrix(self.pattern_graph)
        eigenvalues = np.linalg.eigvals(laplacian.toarray())
        
        # Algebraic connectivity (second smallest eigenvalue)
        sorted_eigenvals = np.sort(np.real(eigenvalues))
        algebraic_connectivity = sorted_eigenvals[1] if len(sorted_eigenvals)
# pattern_processor.py

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
from scipy.sparse.linalg import eigsh
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Set
from dataclasses import dataclass
import logging
from rdkit import Chem
from rdkit.Chem import AllChem, Fragments

@dataclass
class PatternState:
    pattern_id: str
    dimensions: List[int]
    strength: float
    coherence: float
    connections: Set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    evolution_history: List[Dict] = field(default_factory=list)

class DrugPatternProcessor:
    def __init__(self, quantum_engine, dimensions: int = 512):
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.quantum_engine = quantum_engine
        self.pattern_graph = nx.DiGraph()
        self.resonance_field = np.zeros((dimensions, 360), dtype=np.complex128)
        self.stability_threshold = 0.7
        self._initialize_resonance()

    def _initialize_resonance(self):
        """Initialize quantum resonance fields"""
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            angle_space = np.linspace(0, 2*np.pi, 360)
            self.resonance_field[dim] = np.exp(1j * angle_space)

    def process_molecule(self, mol_data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Process molecular structure for pattern detection"""
        mol = Chem.MolFromSmiles(mol_data['smiles'])
        if not mol:
            raise ValueError("Invalid molecular structure")

        # Extract quantum features
        quantum_features = self._extract_quantum_features(mol)
        
        # Detect resonance patterns
        resonances = self._detect_resonance_patterns(quantum_features)
        
        # Calculate pharmacophore features
        pharmacophores = self._identify_pharmacophores(mol)
        
        # Analyze binding sites
        binding_sites = self._analyze_binding_sites(mol, resonances)

        return {
            'quantum_features': quantum_features,
            'resonance_patterns': resonances,
            'pharmacophores': pharmacophores,
            'binding_sites': binding_sites,
            'graph_state': self._get_graph_state()
        }

    def _extract_quantum_features(self, mol) -> np.ndarray:
        """Extract quantum-inspired molecular features"""
        conf = mol.GetConformer()
        features = np.zeros((self.dimensions, 4), dtype=np.complex128)

        for atom in mol.GetAtoms():
            idx = atom.GetIdx()
            if idx < self.dimensions:
                pos = conf.GetAtomPosition(idx)
                # Quantum state encoding
                features[idx] = [
                    atom.GetAtomicNum() * np.exp(1j * pos.x),
                    atom.GetDegree() * np.exp(1j * pos.y),
                    atom.GetFormalCharge() * np.exp(1j * pos.z),
                    atom.GetIsAromatic() * np.exp(1j * np.pi)
                ]

        return features

    def _detect_resonance_patterns(self, quantum_features: np.ndarray) -> List[Dict]:
        """Detect quantum resonance patterns"""
        patterns = []
        
        # Calculate resonance field interactions
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            field = np.dot(quantum_features[dim], self.resonance_field[dim])
            
            # Find strong resonances
            resonance_strengths = np.abs(field)
            significant_idx = np.where(resonance_strengths > self.stability_threshold)[0]
            
            for idx in significant_idx:
                pattern = {
                    'dimension': dim,
                    'strength': float(resonance_strengths[idx]),
                    'phase': float(np.angle(field[idx])),
                    'frequency': idx / 360.0 * 2 * np.pi
                }
                patterns.append(pattern)
        
        return patterns

    def _identify_pharmacophores(self, mol) -> List[Dict]:
        """Identify pharmacophore patterns with quantum properties"""
        pharmacophores = []
        
        # Define pharmacophore features with quantum characteristics
        features = {
            'donor': '[!#6;!H0]',
            'acceptor': '[$([!#6;+0]);!$([F,Cl,Br,I])]',
            'aromatic': '[a]',
            'hydrophobic': '[#6,#7,#8][CX4H3,CX4H2]'
        }

        for name, smarts in features.items():
            pattern = Chem.MolFromSmarts(smarts)
            matches = mol.GetSubstructMatches(pattern)
            
            for match in matches:
                quantum_props = self._calculate_quantum_properties(mol, match)
                pharmacophores.append({
                    'type': name,
                    'atoms': list(match),
                    'quantum_state': quantum_props
                })

        return pharmacophores

    def _calculate_quantum_properties(self, mol, atoms: tuple) -> Dict:
        """Calculate quantum properties for atom group"""
        properties = {}
        
        # Calculate electron density
        electron_density = sum(mol.GetAtomWithIdx(i).GetAtomicNum() for i in atoms)
        
        # Calculate spatial phase
        conf = mol.GetConformer()
        positions = [conf.GetAtomPosition(i) for i in atoms]
        center = np.mean(positions, axis=0)
        phase = np.arctan2(center.y, center.x)
        
        # Quantum numbers
        properties['electron_density'] = electron_density
        properties['phase'] = float(phase)
        properties['coherence'] = float(np.exp(-np.std([p.z for p in positions])))
        
        return properties

    def _analyze_binding_sites(self, mol, resonances: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:
        """Analyze potential binding sites using quantum resonance"""
        binding_sites = []
        
        # Find regions of high resonance
        for resonance in resonances:
            if resonance['strength'] > 0.8:  # Strong resonance threshold
                site = {
                    'center': resonance['dimension'],
                    'strength': resonance['strength'],
                    'accessibility': self._calculate_accessibility(mol, resonance)
                }
                binding_sites.append(site)
        
        return binding_sites

    def _calculate_accessibility(self, mol, resonance: Dict) -> float:
        """Calculate binding site accessibility"""
        exposure = 0.0
        center_idx = resonance['dimension']
        if center_idx < mol.GetNumAtoms():
            atom = mol.GetAtomWithIdx(center_idx)
            # Calculate solvent exposure
            exposure = 1.0 - (atom.GetDegree() / 4.0)  # Normalize by typical max degree
            # Modify by resonance strength
            exposure *= resonance['strength']
        return float(exposure)

    def _get_graph_state(self) -> Dict:
        """Get current state of pattern graph"""
        return {
            'num_patterns': len(self.pattern_graph),
            'edge_density': nx.density(self.pattern_graph),
            'clustering': nx.average_clustering(self.pattern_graph),
            'centrality': nx.eigenvector_centrality_numpy(self.pattern_graph)
        }

# pattern-recognition.py


    def _identify_pharmacophores(self, mol) -> List[Dict]:
        """Identify pharmacophore patterns in molecule"""
        pharmacophores = []
        
        # Define pharmacophore feature definitions
        feature_definitions = {
            'h_donor': '[!#6;!H0]',
            'h_acceptor': '[$([!#6;+0]);!$([F,Cl,Br,I]);!$([o,s,nX3]);!$([Nv5,Pv5,Sv4,Sv6])]',
            'aromatic': '[a]',
            'positive': '[+,+2,+3,+4]',
            'negative': '[-,-2,-3,-4]',
            'hydrophobic': '[#6,#7,#8][CX4H3,CX4H2,CX4H1,CX4H0]',
            'ring': '[R]'
        }
        
        for feature_name, smarts in feature_definitions.items():
            pattern = Chem.MolFromSmarts(smarts)
            matches = mol.GetSubstructMatches(pattern)
            
            for match in matches:
                environment = self._analyze_feature_environment(mol, match)
                vectors = self._calculate_feature_vectors(mol, match)
                
                pharmacophore = {
                    'type': feature_name,
                    'atoms': list(match),
                    'environment': environment,
                    'vectors': vectors,
                    'score': self._calculate_feature_score(environment, vectors)
                }
                pharmacophores.append(pharmacophore)
        
        # Group interacting pharmacophores
        pharmacophore_groups = self._group_interacting_pharmacophores(pharmacophores, mol)
        return self._rank_pharmacophores(pharmacophore_groups)

    def _analyze_feature_environment(self, mol, atom_indices: tuple) -> Dict:
        """Analyze chemical environment around pharmacophore feature"""
        env = {'atoms': [], 'properties': {}}
        radius = 3  # Analysis radius in bonds
        
        for center_idx in atom_indices:
            env_atoms = set()
            for radius_i in range(1, radius + 1):
                env_atoms.update(
                    [x[0] for x in Chem.FindAtomEnvironmentOfRadiusN(mol, radius_i, center_idx)]
                )
            
            for atom_idx in env_atoms:
                atom = mol.GetAtomWithIdx(atom_idx)
                env['atoms'].append({
                    'index': atom_idx,
                    'element': atom.GetSymbol(),
                    'aromatic': atom.GetIsAromatic(),
                    'hybridization': str(atom.GetHybridization()),
                    'formal_charge': atom.GetFormalCharge()
                })
        
        # Calculate environment properties
        env['properties'] = {
            'local_logp': Fragments.fr_logp(mol, atoms=list(env_atoms)),
            'e_state': self._calculate_estate_indices(mol, list(env_atoms)),
            'topology': self._analyze_local_topology(mol, list(env_atoms))
        }
        
        return env

    def _calculate_feature_vectors(self, mol, atom_indices: tuple) -> Dict:
        """Calculate geometric and interaction vectors for pharmacophore features"""
        conf = mol.GetConformer()
        center = np.mean([conf.GetAtomPosition(i) for i in atom_indices], axis=0)
        
        vectors = {
            'position': center,
            'normals': [],
            'directions': []
        }
        
        # Calculate feature direction vectors
        if len(atom_indices) > 1:
            points = [conf.GetAtomPosition(i) for i in atom_indices]
            vectors['directions'] = self._calculate_principal_axes(points)
        
        # Calculate surface normals for planar features
        if len(atom_indices) > 2:
            plane_atoms = [conf.GetAtomPosition(i) for i in atom_indices[:3]]
            normal = np.cross(
                plane_atoms[1] - plane_atoms[0],
                plane_atoms[2] - plane_atoms[0]
            )
            vectors['normals'].append(normal / np.linalg.norm(normal))
        
        return vectors

    def _calculate_estate_indices(self, mol, atom_indices: List[int]) -> np.ndarray:
        """Calculate electrotopological state indices for atoms"""
        from rdkit.Chem import EState
        
        estate_vs = []
        for idx in atom_indices:
            atom = mol.GetAtomWithIdx(idx)
            estate = EState.EStateIndices(mol)[idx]
            estate_vs.append(estate)
            
        return np.array(estate_vs)

    def _analyze_local_topology(self, mol, atom_indices: List[int]) -> Dict:
        """Analyze topological properties of local environment"""
        local_graph = nx.Graph()
        
        # Build local graph
        for idx in atom_indices:
            atom = mol.GetAtomWithIdx(idx)
            for neighbor in atom.GetNeighbors():
                n_idx = neighbor.GetIdx()
                if n_idx in atom_indices:
                    local_graph.add_edge(idx, n_idx)
        
        return {
            'clustering': nx.average_clustering(local_graph),
            'centrality': nx.betweenness_centrality(local_graph),
            'cycles': list(nx.cycle_basis(local_graph))
        }

    def _calculate_principal_axes(self, points: List) -> np.ndarray:
        """Calculate principal axes of point set using SVD"""
        points = np.array(points)
        centered = points - np.mean(points, axis=0)
        _, _, vh = np.linalg.svd(centered)
        return vh

    def _calculate_feature_score(self, environment: Dict, vectors: Dict) -> float:
        """Calculate pharmacophore feature importance score"""
        # Environment score components
        env_score = np.mean([
            len(environment['atoms']) / 10.0,  # Size of environment
            abs(environment['properties']['local_logp']) / 5.0,  # Hydrophobic contribution
            np.mean(environment['properties']['e_state']) / 10.0  # Electronic state contribution
        ])
        
        # Geometric score components
        geo_score = 0.0
        if vectors['normals']:
            geo_score += np.mean([np.linalg.norm(n) for n in vectors['normals']])
        if vectors['directions']:
            geo_score += len(vectors['directions']) / 3.0
            
        return 0.4 * env_score + 0.6 * geo_score

    def _group_interacting_pharmacophores(self, pharmacophores: List[Dict], mol) -> List[List[Dict]]:
        """Group pharmacophores that can form interaction patterns"""
        groups = []
        used = set()
        
        for i, p1 in enumerate(pharmacophores):
            if i in used:
                continue
                
            current_group = [p1]
            used.add(i)
            
            for j, p2 in enumerate(pharmacophores[i+1:], i+1):
                if j in used:
                    continue
                    
                if self._check_pharmacophore_interaction(p1, p2, mol):
                    current_group.append(p2)
                    used.add(j)
                    
            groups.append(current_group)
            
        return groups

    def _check_pharmacophore_interaction(self, p1: Dict, p2: Dict, mol) -> bool:
        """Check if two pharmacophores can form meaningful interactions"""
        # Distance check
        conf = mol.GetConformer()
        pos1 = np.mean([conf.GetAtomPosition(i) for i in p1['atoms']], axis=0)
        pos2 = np.mean([conf.GetAtomPosition(i) for i in p2['atoms']], axis=0)
        distance = np.linalg.norm(pos1 - pos2)
        
        if distance > 8.0:  # Maximum interaction distance in Angstroms
            return False
            
        # Interaction compatibility check
        compatible_pairs = {
            ('h_donor', 'h_acceptor'),
            ('positive', 'negative'),
            ('aromatic', 'aromatic'),
            ('hydrophobic', 'hydrophobic')
        }
        
        interaction_types = tuple(sorted([p1['type'], p2['type']]))
        return interaction_types in compatible_pairs

    def _rank_pharmacophores(self, pharmacophore_groups: List[List[Dict]]) -> List[Dict]:
        """Rank pharmacophore groups by importance"""
        ranked_groups = []
        
        for group in pharmacophore_groups:
            group_score = np.mean([p['score'] for p in group])
            interaction_score = self._calculate_interaction_score(group)
            total_score = 0.7 * group_score + 0.3 * interaction_score
            
            ranked_groups.append({
                'pharmacophores': group,
                'score': total_score,
                'size': len(group),
                'interaction_patterns': self._identify_interaction_patterns(group)
            })
            
        return sorted(ranked_groups, key=lambda x: x['score'], reverse=True)

    def _calculate_interaction_score(self, group: List[Dict]) -> float:
        """Calculate interaction score for a pharmacophore group"""
        if len(group) < 2:
            return 0.0
            
        # Calculate pairwise interaction scores
        interaction_scores = []
        for i, p1 in enumerate(group):
            for p2 in group[i+1:]:
                # Distance score
                dist = np.linalg.norm(p1['vectors']['position'] - p2['vectors']['position'])
                dist_score = np.exp(-dist / 4.0)  # Distance decay factor
                
                # Orientation score
                orient_score = 1.0
                if p1['vectors']['normals'] and p2['vectors']['normals']:
                    angle = np.arccos(np.clip(
                        np.dot(p1['vectors']['normals'][0], p2['vectors']['normals'][0]),
                        -1.0, 1.0
                    ))
                    orient_score = np.cos(angle) ** 2
                    
                interaction_scores.append(dist_score * orient_score)
                
        return np.mean(interaction_scores) if interaction_scores else 0.0

    def _identify_interaction_patterns(self, group: List[Dict]) -> List[str]:
        """Identify specific interaction patterns in pharmacophore group"""
        patterns = []
        
        # Check for common binding patterns
        if len(group) >= 3:
            types = [p['type'] for p in group]
            
            pattern_definitions = {
                'hbond_network': (
                    lambda x: x.count('h_donor') >= 1 and x.count('h_acceptor') >= 2
                ),
                'charge_clamp': (
                    lambda x: 'positive' in x and 'negative' in x
                ),
                'aromatic_cage': (
                    lambda x: x.count('aromatic') >= 3
                ),
                'hydrophobic_patch': (
                    lambda x: x.count('hydrophobic') >= 3
                )
            }
            
            for pattern_name, check_fn in pattern_definitions.items():
                if check_fn(types):
                    patterns.append(pattern_name)
                    
        return patterns

    def _update_pattern_graph(self, pattern_vectors: np.ndarray, pharmacophores: List[Dict]):
        """Update pattern recognition graph with new observations"""
        # Add new pattern nodes
        for i, vector in enumerate(pattern_vectors):
            node_id = f"pattern_{len(self.pattern_graph)}"
            self.pattern_graph.add_node(
                node_id,
                vector=vector,
                type='substructure'
            )
            self.pattern_embeddings[node_id] = vector
            
        # Add pharmacophore pattern nodes
        for pharm in pharmacophores:
            node_id = f"pharm_{len(self.pattern_graph)}"
            vector = self._generate_pharmacophore_vector(pharm)
            self.pattern_graph.add_node(
                node_id,
                vector=vector,
                type='pharmacophore'
            )
            self.pattern_embeddings[node_id] = vector
            
        # Update edges based on pattern similarities
        self._update_pattern_edges()

    def _generate_pharmacophore_vector(self, pharmacophore: Dict) -> np.ndarray:
        """Generate vector representation of pharmacophore"""
        # Combine feature properties into vector
        properties = []
        
        # Type encoding
        type_vec = np.zeros(7)  # One-hot encoding of pharmacophore types
        type_idx = list(self.feature_definitions.keys()).index(pharmacophore['type'])
        type_vec[type_idx] = 1
        properties.extend(type_vec)
        
        # Environment properties
        env = pharmacophore['environment']
        properties.extend([
            len(env['atoms']),
            env['properties']['local_logp'],
            np.mean(env['properties']['e_state'])
        ])
        
        # Geometric properties
        vectors = pharmacophore['vectors']
        if vectors['normals']:
            properties.extend(vectors['normals'][0])
        else:
            properties.extend([0, 0, 0])
            
        # Project to embedding dimension
        return self._project_to_embedding(np.array(properties))

    def _update_pattern_edges(self):
        """Update edges in pattern graph based on vector similarities"""
        nodes = list(self.pattern_graph.nodes())
        for i, node1 in enumerate(nodes):
            vec1 = self.pattern_embeddings[node1]
            
            for node2 in nodes[i+1:]:
                vec2 = self.pattern_embeddings[node2]
                similarity = np.dot(vec1, vec2)
                
                if similarity > 0.7:  # Similarity threshold
                    self.pattern_graph.add_edge(
                        node1,
                        node2,
                        weight=float(similarity)
                    )

    def _calculate_graph_metrics(self) -> Dict:
        """Calculate pattern graph metrics"""
        return {
            'num_patterns': self.pattern_graph.number_of_nodes(),
            'num_connections': self.pattern_graph.number_of_edges(),
            'avg_degree': np.mean([d for _, d in self.pattern_graph.degree()]),
            'clustering': nx.average_clustering(self.pattern_graph),
            'components': nx.number_connected_components(self.pattern_graph)
        }

    def _project_to_embedding(self, features: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Project feature vector to embedding space"""
        if not hasattr(self, 'projection_matrix'):
            max_features = 1000  # Maximum expected feature dimension
            self.projection_matrix = np.random.normal(
                0, 1/np.sqrt(self.embedding_dim),
                (max_features, self.embedding_dim)
            )
            
        padded_features = np.pad(
            features,
            (0, self.projection_matrix.shape[0] - len(features))
        )
        return np.dot(padded_features, self.projection_matrix)

# pattern_recognition.py

# pattern_recognition.py

from typing import Dict, List, Tuple
import numpy as np

class PatternRecognizer:
    """Identifies and correlates patterns across text and visual modalities"""

    def __init__(self):
        self.text_patterns = {}  # Stores text-based patterns
        self.visual_patterns = {}  # Stores visual-based patterns

    def correlate_patterns(
        self, text_patterns: Dict, visual_patterns: Dict
    ) -> List[Tuple[str, str, float]]:
        """
        Correlates text and visual patterns based on shared features.
        Returns a list of tuples: (text_id, visual_id, similarity_score)
        """
        correlations = []

        for text_id, text_feature in text_patterns.items():
            for visual_id, visual_feature in visual_patterns.items():
                similarity = 1 - np.mean(np.abs(text_feature - visual_feature))
                if similarity > 0.7:  # Threshold for correlation
                    correlations.append((text_id, visual_id, similarity))

        return correlations

    def add_text_pattern(self, pattern_id: str, features: np.ndarray):
        """Adds a text-based pattern to the database"""
        self.text_patterns[pattern_id] = features

    def add_visual_pattern(self, pattern_id: str, features: np.ndarray):
        """Adds a visual-based pattern to the database"""
        self.visual_patterns[pattern_id] = features

# PatternRecognition.py


import numpy as np
from scipy import signal

class PatternRecognition:
    def __init__(self):
        self.patterns = {}

    def detect_trends(self, data):
        """Detect trends in the data using polynomial fitting."""
        trend = np.polyfit(range(len(data)), data, 2)
        print(f"Detected trend coefficients: {trend}")
        return trend

    def detect_frequency_peaks(self, data, sampling_rate=1.0):
        """Detect dominant frequencies in the data using FFT."""
        fft = np.fft.fft(data)
        freqs = np.fft.fftfreq(len(data), d=1/sampling_rate)
        peaks = sorted(zip(freqs, np.abs(fft)), key=lambda x: -x[1])[:5]
        print(f"Detected frequency peaks: {peaks}")
        return peaks

    def add_pattern(self, pattern_name, data):
        """Store a recognized pattern for later use."""
        self.patterns[pattern_name] = data
        print(f"Pattern '{pattern_name}' added.")

    def query_pattern(self, pattern_name):
        """Retrieve a stored pattern by name."""
        return self.patterns.get(pattern_name, None)

# Example usage of PatternRecognition
if __name__ == "__main__":
    pr = PatternRecognition()
    sample_data = np.sin(np.linspace(0, 2 * np.pi, 100))
    pr.detect_trends(sample_data)
    pr.detect_frequency_peaks(sample_data)
    pr.add_pattern("SineWave", sample_data)
    print("Stored Pattern:", pr.query_pattern("SineWave"))

# pauli.py

"""Pauli operators and states"""

from sympy.core.add import Add
from sympy.core.mul import Mul
from sympy.core.numbers import I
from sympy.core.power import Pow
from sympy.core.singleton import S
from sympy.functions.elementary.exponential import exp
from sympy.physics.quantum import Operator, Ket, Bra
from sympy.physics.quantum import ComplexSpace
from sympy.matrices import Matrix
from sympy.functions.special.tensor_functions import KroneckerDelta

__all__ = [
    'SigmaX', 'SigmaY', 'SigmaZ', 'SigmaMinus', 'SigmaPlus', 'SigmaZKet',
    'SigmaZBra', 'qsimplify_pauli'
]


class SigmaOpBase(Operator):
    """Pauli sigma operator, base class"""

    @property
    def name(self):
        return self.args[0]

    @property
    def use_name(self):
        return bool(self.args[0]) is not False

    @classmethod
    def default_args(self):
        return (False,)

    def __new__(cls, *args, **hints):
        return Operator.__new__(cls, *args, **hints)

    def _eval_commutator_BosonOp(self, other, **hints):
        return S.Zero


class SigmaX(SigmaOpBase):
    """Pauli sigma x operator

    Parameters
    ==========

    name : str
        An optional string that labels the operator. Pauli operators with
        different names commute.

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy.physics.quantum import represent
    >>> from sympy.physics.quantum.pauli import SigmaX
    >>> sx = SigmaX()
    >>> sx
    SigmaX()
    >>> represent(sx)
    Matrix([
    [0, 1],
    [1, 0]])
    """

    def __new__(cls, *args, **hints):
        return SigmaOpBase.__new__(cls, *args, **hints)

    def _eval_commutator_SigmaY(self, other, **hints):
        if self.name != other.name:
            return S.Zero
        else:
            return 2 * I * SigmaZ(self.name)

    def _eval_commutator_SigmaZ(self, other, **hints):
        if self.name != other.name:
            return S.Zero
        else:
            return - 2 * I * SigmaY(self.name)

    def _eval_commutator_BosonOp(self, other, **hints):
        return S.Zero

    def _eval_anticommutator_SigmaY(self, other, **hints):
        return S.Zero

    def _eval_anticommutator_SigmaZ(self, other, **hints):
        return S.Zero

    def _eval_adjoint(self):
        return self

    def _print_contents_latex(self, printer, *args):
        if self.use_name:
            return r'{\sigma_x^{(%s)}}' % str(self.name)
        else:
            return r'{\sigma_x}'

    def _print_contents(self, printer, *args):
        return 'SigmaX()'

    def _eval_power(self, e):
        if e.is_Integer and e.is_positive:
            return SigmaX(self.name).__pow__(int(e) % 2)

    def _represent_default_basis(self, **options):
        format = options.get('format', 'sympy')
        if format == 'sympy':
            return Matrix([[0, 1], [1, 0]])
        else:
            raise NotImplementedError('Representation in format ' +
                                      format + ' not implemented.')


class SigmaY(SigmaOpBase):
    """Pauli sigma y operator

    Parameters
    ==========

    name : str
        An optional string that labels the operator. Pauli operators with
        different names commute.

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy.physics.quantum import represent
    >>> from sympy.physics.quantum.pauli import SigmaY
    >>> sy = SigmaY()
    >>> sy
    SigmaY()
    >>> represent(sy)
    Matrix([
    [0, -I],
    [I,  0]])
    """

    def __new__(cls, *args, **hints):
        return SigmaOpBase.__new__(cls, *args)

    def _eval_commutator_SigmaZ(self, other, **hints):
        if self.name != other.name:
            return S.Zero
        else:
            return 2 * I * SigmaX(self.name)

    def _eval_commutator_SigmaX(self, other, **hints):
        if self.name != other.name:
            return S.Zero
        else:
            return - 2 * I * SigmaZ(self.name)

    def _eval_anticommutator_SigmaX(self, other, **hints):
        return S.Zero

    def _eval_anticommutator_SigmaZ(self, other, **hints):
        return S.Zero

    def _eval_adjoint(self):
        return self

    def _print_contents_latex(self, printer, *args):
        if self.use_name:
            return r'{\sigma_y^{(%s)}}' % str(self.name)
        else:
            return r'{\sigma_y}'

    def _print_contents(self, printer, *args):
        return 'SigmaY()'

    def _eval_power(self, e):
        if e.is_Integer and e.is_positive:
            return SigmaY(self.name).__pow__(int(e) % 2)

    def _represent_default_basis(self, **options):
        format = options.get('format', 'sympy')
        if format == 'sympy':
            return Matrix([[0, -I], [I, 0]])
        else:
            raise NotImplementedError('Representation in format ' +
                                      format + ' not implemented.')


class SigmaZ(SigmaOpBase):
    """Pauli sigma z operator

    Parameters
    ==========

    name : str
        An optional string that labels the operator. Pauli operators with
        different names commute.

    Examples
    ========

    >>> from sympy.physics.quantum import represent
    >>> from sympy.physics.quantum.pauli import SigmaZ
    >>> sz = SigmaZ()
    >>> sz ** 3
    SigmaZ()
    >>> represent(sz)
    Matrix([
    [1,  0],
    [0, -1]])
    """

    def __new__(cls, *args, **hints):
        return SigmaOpBase.__new__(cls, *args)

    def _eval_commutator_SigmaX(self, other, **hints):
        if self.name != other.name:
            return S.Zero
        else:
            return 2 * I * SigmaY(self.name)

    def _eval_commutator_SigmaY(self, other, **hints):
        if self.name != other.name:
            return S.Zero
        else:
            return - 2 * I * SigmaX(self.name)

    def _eval_anticommutator_SigmaX(self, other, **hints):
        return S.Zero

    def _eval_anticommutator_SigmaY(self, other, **hints):
        return S.Zero

    def _eval_adjoint(self):
        return self

    def _print_contents_latex(self, printer, *args):
        if self.use_name:
            return r'{\sigma_z^{(%s)}}' % str(self.name)
        else:
            return r'{\sigma_z}'

    def _print_contents(self, printer, *args):
        return 'SigmaZ()'

    def _eval_power(self, e):
        if e.is_Integer and e.is_positive:
            return SigmaZ(self.name).__pow__(int(e) % 2)

    def _represent_default_basis(self, **options):
        format = options.get('format', 'sympy')
        if format == 'sympy':
            return Matrix([[1, 0], [0, -1]])
        else:
            raise NotImplementedError('Representation in format ' +
                                      format + ' not implemented.')


class SigmaMinus(SigmaOpBase):
    """Pauli sigma minus operator

    Parameters
    ==========

    name : str
        An optional string that labels the operator. Pauli operators with
        different names commute.
