        self.basis_functions = self._initialize_basis_functions()
        
    def analyze_electronic_structure(self, molecule_data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """
        Analyze electronic structure of molecule.
        """
        try:
            # Generate Hamiltonian matrix
            hamiltonian = self._build_hamiltonian(molecule_data)
            
            # Solve Schrödinger equation
            energies, wavefunctions = eigh(hamiltonian)
            
            # Calculate electron density
            density = self._calculate_electron_density(wavefunctions)
            
            # Analyze molecular orbitals
            orbital_analysis = self._analyze_molecular_orbitals(energies, wavefunctions)
            
            return {
                'energies': energies.tolist(),
                'electron_density': density.tolist(),
                'orbital_analysis': orbital_analysis,
                'bond_orders': self._calculate_bond_orders(density),
                'charge_distribution': self._analyze_charge_distribution(density)
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Error in quantum analysis: {str(e)}")
            raise
            
    def _build_hamiltonian(self, molecule_data: Dict) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Build Hamiltonian matrix using semi-empirical parameters.
        """
        atoms = molecule_data['atoms']
        coordinates = molecule_data['coordinates']
        n_basis = len(self.basis_functions)
        
        H = np.zeros((n_basis, n_basis))
        
        # Add kinetic energy terms
        for i in range(n_basis):
            H[i,i] = self.basis_functions[i]['energy']
            
        # Add potential energy terms
        for i in range(n_basis):
            for j in range(i+1, n_basis):
                overlap = self._calculate_overlap(
                    self.basis_functions[i],
                    self.basis_functions[j],
                    coordinates
                )
                H[i,j] = H[j,i] = overlap
                
        return H
        
    def _calculate_electron_density(self, wavefunctions: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Calculate electron density from wavefunctions.
        """
        # Use only occupied orbitals
        occupied = wavefunctions[:, :self.n_electrons//2]
        return np.dot(occupied, occupied.T)
        
    def _analyze_molecular_orbitals(self, 
                                  energies: np.ndarray, 
                                  wavefunctions: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """
        Analyze molecular orbital properties.
        """
        homo_idx = self.n_electrons // 2 - 1
        lumo_idx = homo_idx + 1
        
        return {
            'homo_energy': float(energies[homo_idx]),
            'lumo_energy': float(energies[lumo_idx]),
            'gap': float(energies[lumo_idx] - energies[homo_idx]),
            'orbital_coefficients': {
                'homo': wavefunctions[:, homo_idx].tolist(),
                'lumo': wavefunctions[:, lumo_idx].tolist()
            }
        }
        
    def _calculate_bond_orders(self, density: np.ndarray) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        Calculate bond orders from electron density.
        """
        bond_orders = []
        n_atoms = len(density)
        
        for i in range(n_atoms):
            for j in range(i+1, n_atoms):
                order = np.abs(density[i,j])
                if order > 0.1:  # Bond order threshold
                    bond_orders.append({
                        'atoms': (i, j),
                        'order': float(order)
                    })
                    
        return bond_orders
        
    def _analyze_charge_distribution(self, density: np.ndarray) -> List[float]:
        """
        Analyze charge distribution on atoms.
        """
        # Calculate Mulliken charges
        charges = np.zeros(len(density))
        for i in range(len(density)):
            charges[i] = sum(density[i,:])
        return charges.tolist()
        
    def _initialize_basis_functions(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        Initialize basis functions for calculations.
        """
        # Simple minimal basis set
        return [
            {'type': '1s', 'energy': -13.6},  # Hydrogen 1s
            {'type': '2s', 'energy': -24.5},  # First row 2s
            {'type': '2p', 'energy': -13.1},  # First row 2p
        ]
        
    def _calculate_overlap(self, 
                         basis1: Dict, 
                         basis2: Dict, 
                         coordinates: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """
        Calculate overlap integral between basis functions.
        """
        # Simple overlap calculation
        distance = np.linalg.norm(coordinates[basis1['center']] - 
                                coordinates[basis2['center']])
        return np.exp(-distance)
# quantum-analysis.py

# specialized/quantum/quantum_analyzer.py

import numpy as np
from scipy.linalg import eigh
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple
import logging

class QuantumAnalyzer:
    """
    Quantum chemistry analysis module for molecular orbital calculations
    and electronic structure analysis using semi-empirical methods.
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
        self.basis_functions = self._initialize_basis_functions()
        
    def analyze_electronic_structure(self, molecule_data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """
        Analyze electronic structure of molecule.
        """
        try:
            # Generate Hamiltonian matrix
            hamiltonian = self._build_hamiltonian(molecule_data)
            
            # Solve Schrödinger equation
            energies, wavefunctions = eigh(hamiltonian)
            
            # Calculate electron density
            density = self._calculate_electron_density(wavefunctions)
            
            # Analyze molecular orbitals
            orbital_analysis = self._analyze_molecular_orbitals(energies, wavefunctions)
            
            return {
                'energies': energies.tolist(),
                'electron_density': density.tolist(),
                'orbital_analysis': orbital_analysis,
                'bond_orders': self._calculate_bond_orders(density),
                'charge_distribution': self._analyze_charge_distribution(density)
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Error in quantum analysis: {str(e)}")
            raise
            
    def _build_hamiltonian(self, molecule_data: Dict) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Build Hamiltonian matrix using semi-empirical parameters.
        """
        atoms = molecule_data['atoms']
        coordinates = molecule_data['coordinates']
        n_basis = len(self.basis_functions)
        
        H = np.zeros((n_basis, n_basis))
        
        # Add kinetic energy terms
        for i in range(n_basis):
            H[i,i] = self.basis_functions[i]['energy']
            
        # Add potential energy terms
        for i in range(n_basis):
            for j in range(i+1, n_basis):
                overlap = self._calculate_overlap(
                    self.basis_functions[i],
                    self.basis_functions[j],
                    coordinates
                )
                H[i,j] = H[j,i] = overlap
                
        return H
        
    def _calculate_electron_density(self, wavefunctions: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Calculate electron density from wavefunctions.
        """
        # Use only occupied orbitals
        occupied = wavefunctions[:, :self.n_electrons//2]
        return np.dot(occupied, occupied.T)
        
    def _analyze_molecular_orbitals(self, 
                                  energies: np.ndarray, 
                                  wavefunctions: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """
        Analyze molecular orbital properties.
        """
        homo_idx = self.n_electrons // 2 - 1
        lumo_idx = homo_idx + 1
        
        return {
            'homo_energy': float(energies[homo_idx]),
            'lumo_energy': float(energies[lumo_idx]),
            'gap': float(energies[lumo_idx] - energies[homo_idx]),
            'orbital_coefficients': {
                'homo': wavefunctions[:, homo_idx].tolist(),
                'lumo': wavefunctions[:, lumo_idx].tolist()
            }
        }
        
    def _calculate_bond_orders(self, density: np.ndarray) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        Calculate bond orders from electron density.
        """
        bond_orders = []
        n_atoms = len(density)
        
        for i in range(n_atoms):
            for j in range(i+1, n_atoms):
                order = np.abs(density[i,j])
                if order > 0.1:  # Bond order threshold
                    bond_orders.append({
                        'atoms': (i, j),
                        'order': float(order)
                    })
                    
        return bond_orders
        
    def _analyze_charge_distribution(self, density: np.ndarray) -> List[float]:
        """
        Analyze charge distribution on atoms.
        """
        # Calculate Mulliken charges
        charges = np.zeros(len(density))
        for i in range(len(density)):
            charges[i] = sum(density[i,:])
        return charges.tolist()
        
    def _initialize_basis_functions(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        Initialize basis functions for calculations.
        """
        # Simple minimal basis set
        return [
            {'type': '1s', 'energy': -13.6},  # Hydrogen 1s
            {'type': '2s', 'energy': -24.5},  # First row 2s
            {'type': '2p', 'energy': -13.1},  # First row 2p
        ]
        
    def _calculate_overlap(self, 
                         basis1: Dict, 
                         basis2: Dict, 
                         coordinates: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """
        Calculate overlap integral between basis functions.
        """
        # Simple overlap calculation
        distance = np.linalg.norm(coordinates[basis1['center']] - 
                                coordinates[basis2['center']])
        return np.exp(-distance)
# quantum_binding_optimizer.py

# quantum_binding_optimizer.py

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from scipy.optimize import minimize
from scipy.spatial.transform import Rotation
import networkx as nx
import logging

class QuantumBindingOptimizer:
    def __init__(self, dimensions: int = 512):
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.interaction_tensor = np.zeros((dimensions, dimensions, dimensions), dtype=np.complex128)
        self.binding_network = nx.Graph()
        self.energy_landscape = {}
        
    async def optimize_binding(self,
                             ligand_state: Dict,
                             target_state: Dict,
                             binding_sites: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
        """Optimize binding pose using quantum mechanics"""
        try:
            # Initialize quantum parameters
            quantum_params = self._initialize_quantum_params(
                ligand_state, target_state
            )
            
            # Optimize each binding site
            site_results = []
            for site in binding_sites:
                result = self._optimize_single_site(
                    quantum_params,
                    site,
                    ligand_state['quantum_features']
                )
                site_results.append(result)
                
            # Select best binding mode
            best_site = max(site_results, key=lambda x: x['binding_score'])
            
            # Update binding network
            self._update_binding_network(best_site)
            
            return {
                'optimal_pose': best_site['pose'],
                'binding_energy': float(best_site['energy']),
                'quantum_coupling': float(best_site['quantum_coupling']),
                'stability_score': float(best_site['stability']),
                'interaction_profile': best_site['interactions']
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logging.error(f"Binding optimization error: {str(e)}")
            raise

    def _initialize_quantum_params(self,
                                 ligand_state: Dict,
                                 target_state: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Initialize quantum mechanical parameters"""
        # Create combined quantum state
        combined_state = np.zeros(self.dimensions, dtype=np.complex128)
        combined_state[:len(ligand_state['quantum_state'])] = ligand_state['quantum_state']
        
        # Calculate interaction basis
        interaction_basis = self._calculate_interaction_basis(
            ligand_state['quantum_features'],
            target_state['quantum_features']
        )
        
        return {
            'combined_state': combined_state,
            'interaction_basis': interaction_basis,
            'ligand_features': ligand_state['quantum_features'],
            'target_features': target_state['quantum_features']
        }

    def _calculate_interaction_basis(self,
                                   ligand_features: np.ndarray,
                                   target_features: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Calculate quantum interaction basis"""
        basis = np.zeros((self.dimensions, self.dimensions), dtype=np.complex128)
        
        for i in range(min(len(ligand_features), self.dimensions)):
            for j in range(min(len(target_features), self.dimensions)):
                # Calculate overlap integral
                overlap = np.sum(ligand_features[i] * np.conj(target_features[j]))
                
                # Phase factor
                phase = np.angle(overlap)
                
                # Construct basis element
                basis[i,j] = np.abs(overlap) * np.exp(1j * phase)
                
        return basis

    def _optimize_single_site(self,
                            quantum_params: Dict,
                            binding_site: Dict,
                            ligand_features: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """Optimize binding at a single site"""
        def objective(params):
            # Extract position and rotation parameters
            position = params[:3]
            rotation = Rotation.from_rotvec(params[3:6]).as_matrix()
            
            # Calculate quantum binding energy
            energy = self._calculate_binding_energy(
                position,
                rotation,
                quantum_params,
                binding_site
            )
            
            # Add quantum mechanical terms
            energy += self._calculate_quantum_terms(
                quantum_params['combined_state'],
                position,
                binding_site
            )
            
            return energy

        # Initial parameters [x, y, z, rx, ry, rz]
        initial_params = np.concatenate([
            binding_site['center'],
            np.zeros(3)  # Initial rotation
        ])
        
        # Optimize
        result = minimize(
            objective,
            initial_params,
            method='L-BFGS-B',
            bounds=[(-10, 10)] * 6
        )
        
        if result.success:
            # Calculate final pose properties
            final_position = result.x[:3]
            final_rotation = Rotation.from_rotvec(result.x[3:6]).as_matrix()
            
            # Calculate interaction profile
            interactions = self._calculate_interactions(
                final_position,
                final_rotation,
                quantum_params,
                binding_site
            )
            
            # Calculate stability
            stability = self._calculate_pose_stability(
                result.x,
                quantum_params,
                binding_site
            )
            
            return {
                'pose': {
                    'position': final_position.tolist(),
                    'rotation': final_rotation.tolist()
                },
                'energy': float(result.fun),
                'quantum_coupling': self._calculate_quantum_coupling(
                    quantum_params,
                    final_position,
                    binding_site
                ),
                'stability': stability,
                'interactions': interactions,
                'binding_score': self._calculate_binding_score(
                    result.fun,
                    stability,
                    interactions
                )
            }
            
        return None

    def _calculate_binding_energy(self,
                                position: np.ndarray,
                                rotation: np.ndarray,
                                quantum_params: Dict,
                                binding_site: Dict) -> float:
        """Calculate quantum mechanical binding energy"""
        # Transform ligand features
        transformed_features = self._transform_features(
            quantum_params['ligand_features'],
            position,
            rotation
        )
        
        # Calculate interaction energy
        energy = 0.0
        for i in range(self.dimensions):
            for j in range(self.dimensions):
                interaction = np.abs(
                    np.vdot(transformed_features[i],
                           quantum_params['target_features'][j])
                )
                energy -= interaction * quantum_params['interaction_basis'][i,j]
                
        return float(energy)

    def _calculate_quantum_terms(self,
                               quantum_state: np.ndarray,
                               position: np.ndarray,
                               binding_site: Dict) -> float:
        """Calculate additional quantum mechanical terms"""
        # Phase coherence term
        phase_term = np.abs(np.mean(np.exp(1j * np.angle(quantum_state))))
        
        # Entanglement term
        entanglement = self._calculate_entanglement(
            quantum_state,
            position,
            binding_site
        )
        
        # Combine terms
        return -0.3 * phase_term - 0.7 * entanglement

    def _calculate_entanglement(self,
                              quantum_state: np.ndarray,
                              position: np.ndarray,
                              binding_site: Dict) -> float:
        """Calculate quantum entanglement contribution"""
        site_indices = np.array(binding_site.get('pocket_atoms', []))
        if len(site_indices) == 0:
            return 0.0
            
        # Calculate reduced density matrix
        rho = np.outer(quantum_state, np.conj(quantum_state))
        rho_reduced = np.zeros((len(site_indices), len(site_indices)),
                             dtype=np.complex128)
        
        for i, idx1 in enumerate(site_indices):
            for j, idx2 in enumerate(site_indices):
                rho_reduced[i,j] = rho[idx1, idx2]
                
        # Calculate von Neumann entropy
        eigenvals = np.linalg.eigvalsh(rho_reduced)
        eigenvals = eigenvals[eigenvals > 1e-10]
        entropy = -np.sum(eigenvals * np.log2(eigenvals))
        
        return float(entropy)

# quantum_chatbot_bridge.py

# quantum_chatbot_bridge.py

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
import torch
from scipy.linalg import expm
import networkx as nx

class QuantumChatBridge:
    def __init__(self, quantum_engine, pattern_processor, drug_system):
        self.quantum_engine = quantum_engine
        self.pattern_processor = pattern_processor
        self.drug_system = drug_system
        self.entanglement_graph = nx.Graph()
        self.resonance_memory = {}
        
    async def process_message(self, message: str, quantum_state: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Process message through quantum filters"""
        # Convert message to quantum representation
        message_state = self._encode_message_state(message)
        
        # Entangle with system quantum state
        entangled_state = self._entangle_states(message_state, quantum_state)
        
        # Detect resonance patterns
        patterns = self.pattern_processor.detect_resonance_patterns(entangled_state)
        
        # Analyze through drug discovery system if relevant
        if self._is_drug_related(patterns):
            drug_analysis = await self.drug_system.analyze_patterns(patterns)
            response = self._generate_drug_response(drug_analysis)
        else:
            response = self._generate_quantum_response(patterns)
            
        return {
            'response': response,
            'quantum_state': entangled_state,
            'patterns': patterns
        }

    def _encode_message_state(self, message: str) -> np.ndarray:
        """Encode message into quantum state vector"""
        # Convert message to numerical features
        features = np.zeros(self.quantum_engine.dimensions)
        
        for i, char in enumerate(message):
            idx = hash(char) % self.quantum_engine.dimensions
            amplitude = np.abs(ord(char)) / 255.0
            phase = (ord(char) % 360) * np.pi / 180
            features[idx] = amplitude * np.exp(1j * phase)
            
        # Normalize quantum state
        features /= np.linalg.norm(features)
        return features

# quantum-chat-processor.py

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModel
import networkx as nx
from scipy.sparse import linalg
import asyncio
from dataclasses import dataclass
import logging

@dataclass
class ChatState:
    dialogue_history: List[Dict]
    quantum_states: Dict[str, np.ndarray]
    entanglement_graph: nx.Graph
    coherence_score: float

class QuantumChatProcessor:
    def __init__(self, 
                quantum_core,
                model_name: str = "microsoft/DialoGPT-medium",
                embedding_dim: int = 768,
                max_context_length: int = 2048):
        self.quantum_core = quantum_core
        self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_name)
        self.language_model = AutoModel.from_pretrained(model_name)
        self.embedding_dim = embedding_dim
        self.max_context_length = max_context_length
        
        # Neural components
        self.quantum_projection = nn.Linear(embedding_dim, quantum_core.dimensions)
        self.quantum_embedding = nn.Linear(quantum_core.dimensions, embedding_dim)
        self.pattern_encoder = self._build_pattern_encoder()
        self.response_decoder = self._build_response_decoder()
        
        # State management
        self.chat_state = ChatState(
            dialogue_history=[],
            quantum_states={},
            entanglement_graph=nx.Graph(),
            coherence_score=1.0
        )
        
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

    def _build_pattern_encoder(self) -> nn.Module:
        """Build neural pattern encoder"""
        return nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(self.embedding_dim * 2, 1024),
            nn.LayerNorm(1024),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Dropout(0.1),
            nn.Linear(1024, 512),
            nn.LayerNorm(512),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Linear(512, self.quantum_core.dimensions)
        )

    def _build_response_decoder(self) -> nn.Module:
        """Build neural response decoder"""
        return nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(self.quantum_core.dimensions + self.embedding_dim, 1024),
            nn.LayerNorm(1024),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Dropout(0.1),
            nn.Linear(1024, 512),
            nn.LayerNorm(512),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Linear(512, self.embedding_dim)
        )

    async def process_message(self, message: str) -> str:
        """Process incoming message and generate response"""
        try:
            # Encode message
            message_embedding = self._encode_message(message)
            
            # Project to quantum space
            quantum_pattern = self._project_to_quantum(message_embedding)
            
            # Process through quantum core
            quantum_result = await self._process_quantum(quantum_pattern)
            
            # Generate response
            response = await self._generate_response(message_embedding, quantum_result)
            
            # Update chat state
            self._update_chat_state(message, response, quantum_result)
            
            return response
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Message processing error: {str(e)}")
            raise

    def _encode_message(self, message: str) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Encode message into neural embedding"""
        # Tokenize
        tokens = self.tokenizer(
            message,
            padding=True,
            truncation=True,
            max_length=self.max_context_length,
            return_tensors="pt"
        )
        
        # Get language model embedding
        with torch.no_grad():
            outputs = self.language_model(**tokens)
            embeddings = outputs.last_hidden_state
            
        # Pool embeddings
        attention_mask = tokens['attention_mask']
        masked_embeddings = embeddings * attention_mask.unsqueeze(-1)
        pooled = masked_embeddings.sum(dim=1) / attention_mask.sum(dim=1, keepdim=True)
        
        return pooled

    def _project_to_quantum(self, embedding: torch.Tensor) -> np.ndarray:
        """Project neural embedding to quantum space"""
        # Get dialogue context
        context_embedding = self._get_dialogue_context()
        
        # Concatenate current embedding with context
        combined = torch.cat([embedding, context_embedding], dim=-1)
        
        # Encode pattern
        quantum_pattern = self.pattern_encoder(combined)
        
        # Convert to numpy and normalize
        pattern = quantum_pattern.detach().numpy()
        pattern /= np.linalg.norm(pattern)
        
        return pattern

    async def _process_quantum(self, pattern: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """Process pattern through quantum core"""
        # Process through quantum resonance
        quantum_result = self.quantum_core.process_pattern(pattern)
        
        # Calculate additional quantum metrics
        quantum_result['coherence'] = self._calculate_quantum_coherence(
            quantum_result['quantum_state']
        )
        
        # Update entanglement graph
        self._update_entanglement_graph(quantum_result['resonances'])
        
        return quantum_result

    def _calculate_quantum_coherence(self, quantum_state: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """Calculate quantum coherence of state"""
        # Construct density matrix
        rho = np.outer(quantum_state, np.conjugate(quantum_state))
        
        # Calculate von Neumann entropy
        eigenvals = np.linalg.eigvalsh(rho)
        eigenvals = eigenvals[eigenvals > 1e-10]
        entropy = -np.sum(eigenvals * np.log2(eigenvals))
        
        # Convert to coherence measure
        coherence = 1.0 - entropy / np.log2(len(quantum_state))
        return float(coherence)

    async def _generate_response(self, 
                               message_embedding: torch.Tensor,
                               quantum_result: Dict) -> str:
        """Generate response using quantum-enhanced language model"""
        # Convert quantum state to embedding
        quantum_embedding = self.quantum_embedding(
            torch.from_numpy(quantum_result['quantum_state']).float()
        )
        
        # Combine with message embedding
        combined = torch.cat([
            message_embedding,
            quantum_embedding.unsqueeze(0)
        ], dim=-1)
        
        # Decode response embedding
        response_embedding = self.response_decoder(combined)
        
        # Generate response tokens
        with torch.no_grad():
            response_ids = self.language_model.generate(
                input_ids=None,
                encoder_hidden_states=response_embedding.unsqueeze(1),
                max_length=100,
                num_beams=5,
                no_repeat_ngram_size=2
            )
            
        # Decode response
        response = self.tokenizer.decode(response_ids[0], skip_special_tokens=True)
        
        return response

    def _get_dialogue_context(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Get neural embedding of dialogue context"""
        if not self.chat_state.dialogue_history:
            return torch.zeros(1, self.embedding_dim)
            
        # Concatenate recent messages
        recent_messages = [
            msg['content'] for msg in self.chat_state.dialogue_history[-5:]
        ]
        context = ' '.join(recent_messages)
        
        # Encode context
        return self._encode_message(context)

    def _update_chat_state(self, message: str, response: str, quantum_result: Dict):
        """Update chat state with new interaction"""
        # Add to dialogue history
        self.chat_state.dialogue_history.append({
            'role': 'user',
            'content': message,
            'timestamp': time.time()
        })
        self.chat_state.dialogue_history.append({
            'role': 'assistant',
            'content': response,
            'timestamp': time.time()
        })
        
        # Update quantum states
        msg_id = str(len(self.chat_state.dialogue_history))
        self.chat_state.quantum_states[msg_id] = quantum_result['quantum_state']
        
        # Update coherence score
        self.chat_state.coherence_score = quantum_result['coherence']

    def _update_entanglement_graph(self, resonances: List[Dict]):
        """Update dialogue entanglement graph"""
        # Add new entanglement edges
        for res1 in resonances:
            for res2 in resonances:
                if res1['dimension'] != res2['dimension']:
                    # Calculate entanglement strength
                    strength = np.sqrt(res1['strength'] * res2['strength'])
                    if strength > 0.5:  # Threshold for entanglement
                        self.chat_state.entanglement_graph.add_edge(
                            res1['dimension'],
                            res2['dimension'],
                            weight=strength
                        )
        
        # Remove weak entanglement
        for u, v, data in list(self.chat_state.entanglement_graph.edges(data=True)):
            if data['weight'] < 0.3:
                self.chat_state.entanglement_graph.remove_edge(u, v)

    def get_state_metrics(self) -> Dict:
        """Get current chat state metrics"""
        return {
            'dialogue_turns': len(self.chat_state.dialogue_history) // 2,
            'quantum_states': len(self.chat_state.quantum_states),
            'entanglement_density': nx.density(self.chat_state.entanglement_graph),
            'coherence_score': self.chat_state.coherence_score,
            'quantum_memory': {
                'state_dims': self.quantum_core.dimensions,
                'active_resonances': len(self.quantum_core.resonance_field)
            }
        }

# quantum_coherence_analyzer.py

# quantum_coherence_analyzer.py

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Set, Tuple
from scipy.linalg import eigh, expm
import networkx as nx
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass
class CoherenceState:
    wavefunction: np.ndarray
    density_matrix: np.ndarray
    phase_factors: np.ndarray
    entanglement_entropy: float
    coherence_metrics: Dict[str, float]

class QuantumCoherenceAnalyzer:
    def __init__(self, dimensions: int = 512):
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.coherence_matrix = np.zeros((dimensions, dimensions), dtype=np.complex128)
        self.phase_memory = np.zeros((dimensions, dimensions), dtype=np.complex128)
        self.interference_graph = nx.Graph()
        self.decoherence_rates = self._initialize_decoherence()
        
    def _initialize_decoherence(self) -> np.ndarray:
        """Initialize quantum decoherence rates"""
        rates = np.zeros(self.dimensions)
        for i in range(self.dimensions):
            # Base decoherence rate
            base_rate = 0.01 + 0.001 * i
            
            # Add quantum fluctuations
            fluctuation = 0.005 * np.random.randn()
            rates[i] = max(0.001, base_rate + fluctuation)
            
        return rates

    async def analyze_coherence(self,
                              quantum_state: np.ndarray,
                              patterns: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
        """Analyze quantum coherence in molecular patterns"""
        try:
            # Calculate density matrix
            density_matrix = self._calculate_density_matrix(quantum_state)
            
            # Calculate phase factors
            phase_factors = self._calculate_phase_factors(quantum_state)
            
            # Analyze pattern coherence
            pattern_coherence = self._analyze_pattern_coherence(
                patterns, phase_factors
            )
            
            # Create coherence state
            coherence_state = CoherenceState(
                wavefunction=quantum_state,
                density_matrix=density_matrix,
                phase_factors=phase_factors,
                entanglement_entropy=self._calculate_entanglement_entropy(
                    density_matrix
                ),
                coherence_metrics=self._calculate_coherence_metrics(
                    density_matrix, pattern_coherence
                )
            )
            
            # Optimize coherence
            optimized_state = self._optimize_coherence(coherence_state)
            
            return {
                'coherence_state': coherence_state,
                'optimized_state': optimized_state,
                'pattern_coherence': pattern_coherence,
                'decoherence_prediction': self._predict_decoherence(
                    coherence_state
                )
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logging.error(f"Coherence analysis error: {str(e)}")
            raise

    def _calculate_density_matrix(self, state: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Calculate quantum density matrix"""
        # Pure state density matrix
        rho = np.outer(state, np.conj(state))
        
        # Apply decoherence effects
        for i in range(self.dimensions):
            for j in range(self.dimensions):
                if i != j:
                    gamma = (self.decoherence_rates[i] + 
                            self.decoherence_rates[j]) / 2
                    rho[i,j] *= np.exp(-gamma)
                    
        return rho

    def _calculate_phase_factors(self, state: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Calculate quantum phase factors between states"""
        phases = np.zeros((self.dimensions, self.dimensions), dtype=np.complex128)
        
        for i in range(self.dimensions):
            for j in range(self.dimensions):
                if i != j and (np.abs(state[i]) > 0 and np.abs(state[j]) > 0):
                    # Calculate phase difference
                    phase_diff = np.angle(state[i]) - np.angle(state[j])
                    
                    # Include quantum memory effects
                    if np.abs(self.phase_memory[i,j]) > 0:
                        memory_phase = np.angle(self.phase_memory[i,j])
                        phase_diff = 0.7 * phase_diff + 0.3 * memory_phase
                        
                    phases[i,j] = np.exp(1j * phase_diff)
                    
        return phases

    def _analyze_pattern_coherence(self,
                                 patterns: List[Dict],
                                 phase_factors: np.ndarray) -> List[Dict]:
        """Analyze coherence between quantum patterns"""
        coherence_patterns = []
        
        for i, pattern1 in enumerate(patterns):
            for j, pattern2 in enumerate(patterns[i+1:], i+1):
                # Calculate pattern overlap
                overlap = self._calculate_pattern_overlap(pattern1, pattern2)
                
                if overlap > 0.1:  # Significant overlap threshold
                    # Calculate coherence metrics
                    coherence = self._calculate_pattern_coherence(
                        pattern1, pattern2, phase_factors
                    )
                    
                    coherence_patterns.append({
                        'patterns': (pattern1['id'], pattern2['id']),
                        'overlap': float(overlap),
                        'coherence': coherence,
                        'stability': self._calculate_coherence_stability(
                            coherence, overlap
                        )
                    })
                    
        return coherence_patterns

    def _calculate_pattern_overlap(self,
                                 pattern1: Dict,
                                 pattern2: Dict) -> float:
        """Calculate quantum overlap between patterns"""
        # Extract pattern features
        features1 = pattern1.get('quantum_features', [])
        features2 = pattern2.get('quantum_features', [])
        
        if not features1 or not features2:
            return 0.0
            
        # Calculate overlap integral
        overlap = 0.0
        for f1, f2 in zip(features1, features2):
            overlap += np.abs(f1 * np.conj(f2))
            
        return float(overlap / max(len(features1), len(features2)))

    def _calculate_pattern_coherence(self,
                                   pattern1: Dict,
                                   pattern2: Dict,
                                   phase_factors: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """Calculate coherence metrics between patterns"""
        # Get pattern indices
        idx1 = pattern1.get('indices', [])
        idx2 = pattern2.get('indices', [])
        
        # Calculate phase coherence
        phase_coherence = 0.0
        count = 0
        
        for i in idx1:
            for j in idx2:
                if i < self.dimensions and j < self.dimensions:
                    phase_coherence += np.abs(phase_factors[i,j])
                    count += 1
                    
        if count > 0:
            phase_coherence /= count
            
        # Calculate interference terms
        interference = self._calculate_interference_terms(
            pattern1, pattern2
        )
        
        return {
            'phase_coherence': float(phase_coherence),
            'interference': interference,
            'total_coherence': float(
                0.6 * phase_coherence +
                0.4 * np.mean(list(interference.values()))
            )
        }

    def _calculate_interference_terms(self,
                                    pattern1: Dict,
                                    pattern2: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Calculate quantum interference terms"""
        interference = {}
        
        # Amplitude interference
        if 'amplitude' in pattern1 and 'amplitude' in pattern2:
            interference['amplitude'] = float(
                np.abs(pattern1['amplitude'] * pattern2['amplitude'])
            )
            
        # Frequency interference
        if 'frequency' in pattern1 and 'frequency' in pattern2:
            freq_diff = abs(pattern1['frequency'] - pattern2['frequency'])
            interference['frequency'] = float(np.exp(-freq_diff))
            
        # Phase interference
        if 'phase' in pattern1 and 'phase' in pattern2:
            phase_diff = abs(pattern1['phase'] - pattern2['phase'])
            interference['phase'] = float(np.cos(phase_diff))
            
        return interference

    def _calculate_coherence_stability(self,
                                     coherence: Dict,
                                     overlap: float) -> float:
        """Calculate stability of quantum coherence"""
        # Phase stability
        phase_stability = coherence['phase_coherence']**2
        
        # Interference stability
        interference_stability = np.mean([
            v**2 for v in coherence['interference'].values()
        ])
        
        # Overlap contribution
        overlap_factor = np.exp(-2 * (1 - overlap))
        
        return float(
            0.4 * phase_stability +
            0.4 * interference_stability +
            0.2 * overlap_factor
        )

# quantum-core-engine.py

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx
from scipy.sparse import linalg
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime

@dataclass
class QuantumState:
    """Quantum-inspired state representation for information processing"""
    density_matrix: np.ndarray
    phase_field: np.ndarray
    coherence: float = 1.0
    entanglement_patterns: Dict[str, np.ndarray] = field(default_factory=dict)

class QuantumCore:
    def __init__(self, dimensions: int = 64):
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.state = QuantumState(
            density_matrix=np.zeros((dimensions, dimensions), dtype=np.complex128),
            phase_field=np.random.random((dimensions, dimensions)) * 2 * np.pi
        )
        self.resonance_network = nx.DiGraph()
        self._initialize_quantum_network()
        
    def _initialize_quantum_network(self):
        """Initialize quantum network with entanglement properties"""
        for i in range(self.dimensions):
            self.resonance_network.add_node(i, quantum_state=np.random.normal(0, 1, size=4))
            
        # Create initial entanglement between dimensions
        for i in range(self.dimensions):
            for j in range(i + 1, self.dimensions):
                if np.random.random() < 0.3:  # 30% chance of entanglement
                    strength = np.random.normal(0, 1) + 1j * np.random.normal(0, 1)
                    self.resonance_network.add_edge(i, j, strength=strength)
                    self.resonance_network.add_edge(j, i, strength=np.conj(strength))

    def process_data(self, data: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """Process data through quantum transformation and resonance detection"""
        transformed_data = self._quantum_transform(data)
        resonance_patterns = self._detect_resonance_patterns(transformed_data)
        self._update_quantum_state(resonance_patterns)
        
        return {
            'transformed_data': transformed_data,
            'resonance_patterns': resonance_patterns,
            'quantum_state': self._get_quantum_metrics()
        }

    def _quantum_transform(self, data: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Apply quantum-inspired transformation to input data"""
        # Apply phase rotation based on current quantum state
        phase_rotation = np.exp(1j * self.state.phase_field)
        transformed = data * phase_rotation
        
        # Apply quantum interference effects
        interference = np.zeros_like(transformed, dtype=np.complex128)
        for i, j in self.resonance_network.edges():
            strength = self.resonance_network[i][j]['strength']
            interference += strength * np.outer(transformed[i], np.conj(transformed[j]))
            
        return transformed + 0.1 * interference

    def _detect_resonance_patterns(self, data: np.ndarray) -> List[Dict]:
        """Detect quantum resonance patterns in transformed data"""
        patterns = []
        eigenvals, eigenvecs = linalg.eigs(np.dot(data, data.conj().T))
        
        for i, (val, vec) in enumerate(zip(eigenvals, eigenvecs.T)):
            if abs(val) > 0.1:  # Significant resonance threshold
                coherence = self._calculate_pattern_coherence(vec)
                pattern = {
                    'type': 'quantum_resonance',
                    'strength': abs(val),
                    'phase': np.angle(val),
                    'coherence': coherence,
                    'dimensions': self._get_active_dimensions(vec),
                    'timestamp': datetime.now()
                }
                patterns.append(pattern)
                
        return patterns

    def _calculate_pattern_coherence(self, pattern: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """Calculate quantum coherence of a resonance pattern"""
        density = np.outer(pattern, pattern.conj())
        return float(np.abs(np.trace(np.dot(density, density))))

    def _get_active_dimensions(self, pattern: np.ndarray) -> List[int]:
        """Get dimensions with significant contribution to the pattern"""
        threshold = 0.1 * np.max(np.abs(pattern))
        return [i for i, val in enumerate(pattern) if abs(val) > threshold]

    def _update_quantum_state(self, patterns: List[Dict]):
        """Update quantum state based on detected resonance patterns"""
        # Update phase field based on pattern phases
        phase_updates = np.zeros_like(self.state.phase_field)
        for pattern in patterns:
            dims = pattern['dimensions']
            phase = pattern['phase']
            strength = pattern['strength']
            for i in dims:
                for j in dims:
                    phase_updates[i, j] += phase * strength
        
        self.state.phase_field += 0.1 * phase_updates
        self.state.phase_field = np.mod(self.state.phase_field, 2 * np.pi)
        
        # Update entanglement patterns
        for pattern in patterns:
            self.state.entanglement_patterns[f"pattern_{len(self.state.entanglement_patterns)}"] = \
                pattern['strength'] * np.exp(1j * pattern['phase'])

    def _get_quantum_metrics(self) -> Dict:
        """Calculate quantum state metrics"""
        return {
            'total_coherence': float(np.mean([p['coherence'] for p in self.state.entanglement_patterns.values()])),
            'entanglement_strength': float(nx.density(self.resonance_network)),
            'phase_alignment': float(np.mean(np.exp(1j * self.state.phase_field)))
        }

# quantum-core.py

import numpy as np
from scipy.sparse import linalg
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
import networkx as nx

class QuantumResonanceCore:
    def __init__(self, dimensions: int = 512, coherence_threshold: float = 0.7):
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.coherence_threshold = coherence_threshold
        self.state_matrix = np.zeros((dimensions, dimensions), dtype=np.complex128)
        self.entanglement_graph = nx.Graph()
        self.resonance_field = np.zeros((dimensions, 360), dtype=np.complex128)
        self._initialize_quantum_state()
    
    def _initialize_quantum_state(self):
        """Initialize quantum state with superposition and entanglement"""
        # Create initial superposition state
        for i in range(self.dimensions):
            phase = 2 * np.pi * i / self.dimensions
            self.state_matrix[i, i] = np.exp(1j * phase)
            
        # Initialize resonance fields
        angle_space = np.linspace(0, 2*np.pi, 360)
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            self.resonance_field[dim] = np.exp(1j * (dim + 1) * angle_space)
        
        # Create initial entanglement
        for i in range(self.dimensions):
            for j in range(i+1, self.dimensions):
                if np.random.random() < 0.1:  # 10% initial entanglement probability
                    strength = np.random.random()
                    self.entanglement_graph.add_edge(i, j, weight=strength)

    def process_pattern(self, pattern_data: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """Process input pattern through quantum resonance"""
        # Project input onto quantum state
        projected_state = self._project_to_quantum_state(pattern_data)
        
        # Evolve quantum state
        evolved_state = self._evolve_quantum_state(projected_state)
        
        # Detect resonance patterns
        resonances = self._detect_resonance_patterns(evolved_state)
        
        # Update entanglement graph
        self._update_entanglement(resonances)
        
        return {
            'resonances': resonances,
            'quantum_state': evolved_state,
            'entanglement_metrics': self._calculate_entanglement_metrics()
        }

    def _project_to_quantum_state(self, pattern_data: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Project classical data onto quantum state space"""
        if len(pattern_data.shape) == 1:
            pattern_data = pattern_data.reshape(-1, 1)
            
        # Ensure proper dimensionality
        if pattern_data.shape[0] > self.dimensions:
            pattern_data = pattern_data[:self.dimensions]
        elif pattern_data.shape[0] < self.dimensions:
            padding = np.zeros((self.dimensions - pattern_data.shape[0], pattern_data.shape[1]))
            pattern_data = np.vstack([pattern_data, padding])
            
        # Project using SVD for dimensional reduction
        U, S, Vh = np.linalg.svd(pattern_data, full_matrices=False)
        projected = U @ np.diag(np.sqrt(S))
        
        # Normalize
        projected /= np.linalg.norm(projected, axis=0)
        
        return projected

    def _evolve_quantum_state(self, state: np.ndarray, steps: int = 100) -> np.ndarray:
        """Evolve quantum state through time"""
        dt = 0.01
        current_state = state.copy()
        
        for _ in range(steps):
            # Apply unitary evolution
            evolution_operator = self._construct_evolution_operator(dt)
            current_state = evolution_operator @ current_state
            
            # Apply entanglement effects
            current_state = self._apply_entanglement(current_state)
            
            # Normalize
            current_state /= np.linalg.norm(current_state, axis=0)
            
        return current_state

    def _construct_evolution_operator(self, dt: float) -> np.ndarray:
        """Construct unitary evolution operator"""
        # Construct Hamiltonian
        H = np.zeros((self.dimensions, self.dimensions), dtype=np.complex128)
        
        # Add kinetic terms
        for i in range(self.dimensions):
            H[i, i] = i + 1  # Energy levels
            
            # Nearest-neighbor coupling
            if i > 0:
                H[i, i-1] = H[i-1, i] = -0.1
                
        # Add entanglement contributions
        for i, j in self.entanglement_graph.edges():
            strength = self.entanglement_graph[i][j]['weight']
            H[i, j] = H[j, i] = -strength
            
        # Construct evolution operator
        U = linalg.expm(-1j * H * dt)
        return U

    def _apply_entanglement(self, state: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Apply entanglement effects to quantum state"""
        entangled_state = state.copy()
        
        for i, j in self.entanglement_graph.edges():
            strength = self.entanglement_graph[i][j]['weight']
            
            # Create entanglement operation
            entangle_op = np.eye(self.dimensions, dtype=np.complex128)
            entangle_op[i, j] = entangle_op[j, i] = strength * np.exp(1j * np.pi/4)
            
            entangled_state = entangle_op @ entangled_state
            
        return entangled_state

    def _detect_resonance_patterns(self, state: np.ndarray) -> List[Dict]:
        """Detect quantum resonance patterns"""
        patterns = []
        
        # Calculate resonance with stored fields
        for dim in range(self.dimensions):
            resonance = np.abs(np.dot(state[:, 0], self.resonance_field[dim]))
            
            # Find peaks in resonance
            peaks = self._find_resonance_peaks(resonance)
            
            for peak in peaks:
                if peak['strength'] > self.coherence_threshold:
                    pattern = {
                        'dimension': dim,
                        'angle': peak['angle'],
                        'strength': float(peak['strength']),
                        'phase': float(np.angle(state[dim, 0])),
                        'coherence': float(self._calculate_coherence(state, dim))
                    }
                    patterns.append(pattern)
                    
        return patterns

    def _find_resonance_peaks(self, resonance: np.ndarray) -> List[Dict]:
        """Find peaks in resonance pattern"""
        peaks = []
        for i in range(1, len(resonance)-1):
            if resonance[i] > resonance[i-1] and resonance[i] > resonance[i+1]:
                peaks.append({
                    'angle': i * 360 / len(resonance),
                    'strength': float(resonance[i])
                })
        return peaks

    def _calculate_coherence(self, state: np.ndarray, dimension: int) -> float:
        """Calculate quantum coherence for a dimension"""
        reduced_density = np.outer(state[:, 0], state[:, 0].conj())
        coherence = np.abs(reduced_density[dimension, dimension])
        return float(coherence)

    def _update_entanglement(self, resonances: List[Dict]):
        """Update entanglement graph based on resonance patterns"""
        # Add new entanglement edges
        for i, res1 in enumerate(resonances):
            for res2 in resonances[i+1:]:
                dim1, dim2 = res1['dimension'], res2['dimension']
                
                # Calculate entanglement strength
                phase_alignment = np.cos(res1['phase'] - res2['phase'])
                strength = np.sqrt(res1['strength'] * res2['strength']) * abs(phase_alignment)
                
                if strength > self.coherence_threshold:
                    self.entanglement_graph.add_edge(dim1, dim2, weight=strength)
                    
        # Decay existing entanglement
        for i, j in list(self.entanglement_graph.edges()):
            self.entanglement_graph[i][j]['weight'] *= 0.95
            if self.entanglement_graph[i][j]['weight'] < 0.1:
                self.entanglement_graph.remove_edge(i, j)

    def _calculate_entanglement_metrics(self) -> Dict:
        """Calculate entanglement metrics"""
        return {
            'total_entanglement': sum(d['weight'] for _, _, d in self.entanglement_graph.edges(data=True)),
            'max_entanglement': max((d['weight'] for _, _, d in self.entanglement_graph.edges(data=True)), default=0),
            'avg_node_degree': np.mean([d for _, d in self.entanglement_graph.degree()]),
            'clustering': nx.average_clustering(self.entanglement_graph, weight='weight'),
            'modularity': self._calculate_modularity()
        }

    def _calculate_modularity(self) -> float:
        """Calculate modularity of entanglement graph"""
        try:
            communities = nx.community.greedy_modularity_communities(self.entanglement_graph, weight='weight')
            return nx.community.modularity(self.entanglement_graph, communities, weight='weight')
        except:
            return 0.0

# quantum_engine (3).py

import logging
from collections import defaultdict

# Configure logging for EnergyManager
logging.basicConfig(
    filename="energy_manager.log",
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s"
)

class EnergyManager:
    def __init__(self, total_energy=1000.0):
        """
        Manages energy distribution and consumption for nodes and supernodes.

        Args:
            total_energy (float): Total energy available to the system.
        """
        self.total_energy = total_energy
        self.node_energy = defaultdict(float)
        self.supernode_energy = defaultdict(float)

    def allocate_node_energy(self, node_id, energy_amount):
        """
        Allocates energy to a specific node.

        Args:
            node_id (str): Unique identifier for the node.
            energy_amount (float): Amount of energy to allocate.

        Returns:
            None
        """
        if self.total_energy >= energy_amount:
            self.node_energy[node_id] += energy_amount
            self.total_energy -= energy_amount
            logging.info(f"Allocated {energy_amount} energy to node {node_id}")
        else:
            logging.warning(f"Insufficient energy to allocate to node {node_id}")

    def allocate_supernode_energy(self, supernode_id, energy_amount):
        """
        Allocates energy to a specific supernode.

        Args:
            supernode_id (str): Unique identifier for the supernode.
            energy_amount (float): Amount of energy to allocate.

        Returns:
            None
        """
        if self.total_energy >= energy_amount:
            self.supernode_energy[supernode_id] += energy_amount
            self.total_energy -= energy_amount
            logging.info(f"Allocated {energy_amount} energy to supernode {supernode_id}")
        else:
            logging.warning(f"Insufficient energy to allocate to supernode {supernode_id}")

    def consume_node_energy(self, node_id, energy_consumed):
        """
        Consumes energy from a specific node.

        Args:
            node_id (str): Unique identifier for the node.
            energy_consumed (float): Amount of energy consumed.

        Returns:
            None
        """
        if self.node_energy[node_id] >= energy_consumed:
            self.node_energy[node_id] -= energy_consumed
            logging.info(f"Node {node_id} consumed {energy_consumed} energy")
        else:
            logging.warning(f"Node {node_id} has insufficient energy")

    def consume_supernode_energy(self, supernode_id, energy_consumed):
        """
        Consumes energy from a specific supernode.

        Args:
            supernode_id (str): Unique identifier for the supernode.
            energy_consumed (float): Amount of energy consumed.

        Returns:
            None
        """
        if self.supernode_energy[supernode_id] >= energy_consumed:
            self.supernode_energy[supernode_id] -= energy_consumed
            logging.info(f"Supernode {supernode_id} consumed {energy_consumed} energy")
        else:
            logging.warning(f"Supernode {supernode_id} has insufficient energy")

    def get_remaining_energy(self):
        """
        Returns the total remaining energy in the system.

        Returns:
            float: Remaining energy.
        """
        return self.total_energy

# Example usage
if __name__ == "__main__":
    energy_manager = EnergyManager(total_energy=1000)
    energy_manager.allocate_node_energy("node1", 100)
    energy_manager.allocate_supernode_energy("supernode1", 200)
    energy_manager.consume_node_energy("node1", 50)
    energy_manager.consume_supernode_energy("supernode1", 100)
    remaining_energy = energy_manager.get_remaining_energy()
    print(f"Remaining energy: {remaining_energy}")

# quantum_engine.py

import numpy as np
from scipy.linalg import expm
import networkx as nx
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass
from rdkit import Chem
from rdkit.Chem import AllChem
import logging

@dataclass
class QuantumState:
    wavefunction: np.ndarray
    energy: float
    phase: float
    entanglement: Dict[Tuple[int, int], complex]

class DrugQuantumEngine:
    def __init__(self, dimensions: int = 512):
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.hamiltonian = self._initialize_hamiltonian()
        self.interaction_tensor = np.zeros((dimensions, dimensions, dimensions), dtype=np.complex128)
        self.resonance_network = nx.Graph()
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

    def _initialize_hamiltonian(self) -> np.ndarray:
        """Initialize quantum Hamiltonian with molecular interactions"""
        H = np.zeros((self.dimensions, self.dimensions), dtype=np.complex128)
        
        # Kinetic energy terms
        for i in range(self.dimensions):
            # Diagonal energy terms
            H[i, i] = 0.5 * (i + 1)
            
            # Nearest-neighbor coupling
            if i > 0:
                coupling = -0.1 * np.exp(-0.05 * i)  # Distance-dependent coupling
                H[i, i-1] = H[i-1, i] = coupling
            
            # Non-local interactions
            for j in range(i + 2, self.dimensions):
                long_range = 0.05 * np.exp(-0.1 * abs(i - j))
                H[i, j] = H[j, i] = long_range

        return H

    def simulate_molecule(self, mol_graph: Dict, steps: int = 1000) -> Dict:
        """Quantum simulation of molecular system"""
        try:
            # Initialize quantum state
            state = self._prepare_initial_state(mol_graph)
            
            # Time evolution
            dt = 0.01
            evolved_states = []
            energies = []
            entanglement_entropy = []
            
            for _ in range(steps):
                # Evolve quantum state
                state = self._time_evolution(state, dt)
                
                # Calculate observables
                energy = self._calculate_energy(state)
                entropy = self._calculate_entanglement_entropy(state)
                
                # Store results
                evolved_states.append(state)
                energies.append(energy)
                entanglement_entropy.append(entropy)
                
                # Update interaction tensor
                self._update_interactions(state)

            return {
                'final_state': evolved_states[-1],
                'energies': np.array(energies),
                'entanglement_entropy': np.array(entanglement_entropy),
                'interactions': self._analyze_interactions()
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Simulation error: {str(e)}")
            raise

    def _prepare_initial_state(self, mol_graph: Dict) -> QuantumState:
        """Prepare initial quantum state from molecular graph"""
        wavefunction = np.zeros(self.dimensions, dtype=np.complex128)
        node_features = mol_graph['x'].numpy()
        edge_index = mol_graph['edge_index'].numpy()
        
        # Encode atomic features into quantum state
        for i, features in enumerate(node_features):
            if i < self.dimensions:
                # Calculate quantum numbers from atomic features
                n = int(features[0]) % (self.dimensions // 4)  # Principal quantum number
                l = int(features[1]) % 4  # Angular momentum
                m = int(features[2]) % (2 * l + 1) if l > 0 else 0  # Magnetic quantum number
                
                # Generate quantum state components
                idx = n * 16 + l * 4 + m
                if idx < self.dimensions:
                    amplitude = np.sqrt(features[3]**2 + features[4]**2)
                    phase = np.arctan2(features[4], features[3])
                    wavefunction[idx] = amplitude * np.exp(1j * phase)

        # Initialize entanglement from molecular bonds
        entanglement = {}
        for i in range(edge_index.shape[1]):
            idx1, idx2 = edge_index[:, i]
            if idx1 < self.dimensions and idx2 < self.dimensions:
                strength = np.random.uniform(0.1, 0.5)
                phase = np.random.uniform(0, 2*np.pi)
                entanglement[(int(idx1), int(idx2))] = strength * np.exp(1j * phase)

        # Normalize wavefunction
        wavefunction /= np.linalg.norm(wavefunction)

        return QuantumState(
            wavefunction=wavefunction,
            energy=self._calculate_energy(wavefunction),
            phase=0.0,
            entanglement=entanglement
        )

    def _time_evolution(self, state: QuantumState, dt: float) -> QuantumState:
        """Evolve quantum state through time"""
        # Construct evolution operator
        U = expm(-1j * self.hamiltonian * dt)
        
        # Evolve wavefunction
        new_wavefunction = np.dot(U, state.wavefunction)
        
        # Update entanglement
        new_entanglement = {}
        for (i, j), ent in state.entanglement.items():
            # Evolve entanglement with phase
            new_phase = np.angle(ent) + dt * self._calculate_phase_evolution(state, i, j)
            new_strength = np.abs(ent) * np.exp(-dt * 0.1)  # Gradual decay
            new_entanglement[(i, j)] = new_strength * np.exp(1j * new_phase)

        return QuantumState(
            wavefunction=new_wavefunction,
            energy=self._calculate_energy(new_wavefunction),
            phase=state.phase + dt * self._calculate_phase_evolution_rate(state),
            entanglement=new_entanglement
        )

    def _calculate_energy(self, wavefunction: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """Calculate energy expectation value"""
        return float(np.real(np.conjugate(wavefunction) @ self.hamiltonian @ wavefunction))

    def _calculate_phase_evolution_rate(self, state: QuantumState) -> float:
        """Calculate phase evolution rate from energy uncertainty"""
        energy_variance = np.real(
            np.conjugate(state.wavefunction) @ (self.hamiltonian @ self.hamiltonian) @ state.wavefunction
        ) - state.energy**2
        return np.sqrt(energy_variance)

    def _calculate_phase_evolution(self, state: QuantumState, i: int, j: int) -> float:
        """Calculate local phase evolution between entangled sites"""
        local_h = self.hamiltonian[i:i+1, j:j+1]
        return float(np.real(np.trace(local_h)))

    def _calculate_entanglement_entropy(self, state: QuantumState) -> float:
        """Calculate von Neumann entropy of reduced density matrix"""
        # Construct density matrix
        rho = np.outer(state.wavefunction, np.conjugate(state.wavefunction))
        
        # Partition system
        partition_size = self.dimensions // 2
        rho_a = np.zeros((partition_size, partition_size), dtype=np.complex128)
        
        # Partial trace over subsystem B
        for i in range(partition_size):
            for j in range(partition_size):
                for k in range(partition_size, self.dimensions):
                    rho_a[i, j] += rho[i, j + k]

        # Calculate von Neumann entropy
        eigenvals = np.linalg.eigvalsh(rho_a)
        eigenvals = eigenvals[eigenvals > 1e-10]
        return float(-np.sum(eigenvals * np.log2(eigenvals)))

    def _update_interactions(self, state: QuantumState):
        """Update three-body interaction tensor"""
        wf = state.wavefunction
        self.interaction_tensor = np.zeros_like(self.interaction_tensor)
        
        for i in range(self.dimensions):
            for j in range(self.dimensions):
                for k in range(self.dimensions):
                    self.interaction_tensor[i, j, k] = wf[i] * np.conjugate(wf[j]) * wf[k]

    def _analyze_interactions(self) -> Dict:
        """Analyze quantum interactions in the system"""
        # Find strongest interactions
        strengths = np.abs(self.interaction_tensor)
        max_interactions = []
        
        for i in range(self.dimensions):
            for j in range(i+1, self.dimensions):
                strength = np.max(strengths[i, j, :])
                if strength > 0.1:  # Threshold for significant interactions
                    max_interactions.append({
                        'sites': (i, j),
                        'strength': float(strength),
                        'phase': float(np.angle(
                            self.interaction_tensor[i, j, np.argmax(strengths[i, j, :])]
                        ))
                    })

        return {
            'max_interactions': max_interactions,
            'total_strength': float(np.sum(strengths)),
            'interaction_entropy': float(-np.sum(
                strengths * np.log2(strengths + 1e-10)
            ))
        }

# quantum_harmonic_analyzer.py

# quantum_harmonic_analyzer.py

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple
from scipy.sparse.linalg import eigsh
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import networkx as nx

class QuantumHarmonicAnalyzer:
    def __init__(self, dimensions: int = 512, max_harmonics: int = 8):
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.max_harmonics = max_harmonics
        self.harmonic_tensor = np.zeros((dimensions, max_harmonics, 3), dtype=np.complex128)
        self.coupling_network = nx.Graph()
        self.resonance_memory = {}
        
    def analyze_harmonics(self, quantum_state: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """Analyze quantum harmonic structure"""
        # Initialize harmonic analysis
        harmonics = []
        
        # Calculate fundamental frequencies
        frequencies = self._calculate_fundamental_frequencies(quantum_state)
        
        # Analyze harmonic series
        for i, freq in enumerate(frequencies):
            if i >= self.max_harmonics:
                break
                
            # Calculate harmonic components
            harmonic_series = self._analyze_harmonic_series(
                quantum_state,
                freq
            )
            
            # Calculate coupling strengths
            coupling = self._calculate_harmonic_coupling(
                harmonic_series,
                quantum_state
            )
            
            # Store in harmonic tensor
            self.harmonic_tensor[i] = self._encode_harmonic_state(
                harmonic_series,
                coupling
            )
            
            harmonics.append({
                'frequency': float(freq),
                'series': harmonic_series,
                'coupling': coupling,
                'coherence': self._calculate_harmonic_coherence(
                    harmonic_series
                )
            })
            
        # Update coupling network
        self._update_coupling_network(harmonics)
        
        return {
            'harmonics': harmonics,
            'resonance_structure': self._analyze_resonance_structure(harmonics),
            'coupling_metrics': self._calculate_coupling_metrics()
        }
        
    def _calculate_fundamental_frequencies(self, state: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """Calculate fundamental frequencies using quantum spectral analysis"""
        # Perform quantum Fourier transform
        spectrum = np.fft.fft(state)
        frequencies = np.fft.fftfreq(len(state))
        
        # Find major frequency components
        significant_freq = []
        threshold = 0.1 * np.max(np.abs(spectrum))
        
        for i, amp in enumerate(np.abs(spectrum)):
            if amp > threshold:
                # Calculate true frequency with quantum corrections
                freq = frequencies[i]
                corrected_freq = freq + self._calculate_quantum_correction(
                    state, freq
                )
                significant_freq.append(corrected_freq)
                
        return np.array(sorted(significant_freq))

# quantum_integration_core.py

# quantum_integration_core.py

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
import torch
from dataclasses import dataclass
import networkx as nx
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
from scipy.sparse.linalg import eigsh

@dataclass
class IntegrationState:
    quantum_state: np.ndarray
    harmonic_patterns: List[Dict]
    resonance_field: np.ndarray
    entanglement_graph: nx.Graph
    confidence: float

class QuantumIntegrationCore:
    def __init__(self, 
                 quantum_engine,
                 pattern_processor,
                 harmonics_analyzer,
                 response_generator):
        self.quantum_engine = quantum_engine
        self.pattern_processor = pattern_processor
        self.harmonics_analyzer = harmonics_analyzer
        self.response_generator = response_generator
        self.integration_graph = nx.DiGraph()
        self.field_memory = {}
        
    async def process_interaction(self, 
                                message_state: Dict,
                                molecular_data: Optional[Dict] = None) -> Dict:
        """Process quantum interaction with optional molecular analysis"""
        try:
            # Initialize integration state
            state = self._initialize_integration_state(message_state)
            
            # Process quantum patterns
            patterns = await self._process_quantum_patterns(state)
            
            # Analyze molecular resonance if data present
            if molecular_data:
                mol_patterns = await self._analyze_molecular_resonance(
                    state, molecular_data
                )
                patterns.extend(mol_patterns)
                
            # Update integration graph
            self._update_integration_graph(patterns)
            
            # Generate response
            response = await self.response_generator.generate_response(
                patterns, state.quantum_state
            )
            
            # Update field memory
            self._update_field_memory(state, patterns)
            
            return {
                'response': response,
                'patterns': patterns,
                'state': self._get_state_summary(state)
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logging.error(f"Integration error: {str(e)}")
            raise

    def _initialize_integration_state(self, message_state: Dict) -> IntegrationState:
        """Initialize quantum integration state"""
        quantum_state = message_state['quantum_state']
        
        # Initialize harmonic patterns
        harmonic_patterns = self.harmonics_analyzer.analyze_harmonics(
            quantum_state
        )
        
        # Calculate resonance field
        resonance_field = np.zeros(
            (self.quantum_engine.dimensions, 360),
            dtype=np.complex128
        )
        for dim in range(self.quantum_engine.dimensions):
            for angle in range(360):
                phase = angle * np.pi / 180
                resonance_field[dim, angle] = quantum_state[dim] * np.exp(1j * phase)
                
        return IntegrationState(
            quantum_state=quantum_state,
            harmonic_patterns=harmonic_patterns,
            resonance_field=resonance_field,
            entanglement_graph=nx.Graph(),
            confidence=1.0
        )

    async def _process_quantum_patterns(self, state: IntegrationState) -> List[Dict]:
        """Process quantum patterns from integration state"""
        patterns = []
        
        # Detect resonance patterns
        resonance_patterns = self.pattern_processor.detect_patterns(
            state.quantum_state
        )
        
        # Analyze harmonic interference
        interference_patterns = self._analyze_interference(
            state.harmonic_patterns,
            resonance_patterns
        )
        
        # Calculate quantum correlations
        correlations = self._calculate_correlations(
            state.quantum_state,
            interference_patterns
        )
        
        # Combine patterns with correlation weights
        for pattern in resonance_patterns + interference_patterns:
            if pattern['strength'] > 0.5:  # Significance threshold
                correlation = correlations.get(pattern['type'], 0.0)
                pattern['correlation'] = correlation
                patterns.append(pattern)
                
        return patterns

    def _analyze_interference(self,
                            harmonic_patterns: List[Dict],
                            resonance_patterns: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:
        """Analyze interference between harmonic and resonance patterns"""
        interference_patterns = []
        
        for h_pattern in harmonic_patterns:
            for r_pattern in resonance_patterns:
                # Calculate interference strength
                interference = self._calculate_pattern_interference(
                    h_pattern, r_pattern
                )
                
                if interference['strength'] > 0.3:  # Interference threshold
                    interference_patterns.append(interference)
                    
        return interference_patterns

    def _calculate_pattern_interference(self,
                                     harmonic: Dict,
                                     resonance: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Calculate interference between harmonic and resonance patterns"""
        # Phase difference
        phase_diff = abs(harmonic['phase'] - resonance['phase'])
        
        # Frequency matching
        freq_ratio = harmonic['frequency'] / (resonance['position'] / 360.0)
        
        # Calculate interference strength
        strength = np.exp(-phase_diff) * np.exp(-(freq_ratio - 1)**2)
        
        return {
            'type': 'interference',
            'strength': float(strength),
            'harmonic_freq': harmonic['frequency'],
            'resonance_pos': resonance['position'],
            'phase_coherence': float(np.cos(phase_diff))
        }

    def _calculate_correlations(self,
                              quantum_state: np.ndarray,
                              patterns: List[Dict]) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """Calculate quantum correlations between patterns"""
        correlations = {}
        pattern_states = {}
        
        # Generate pattern states
        for pattern in patterns:
            state = self._generate_pattern_state(pattern)
            pattern_states[pattern['type']] = state
            
        # Calculate correlations
        for type1, state1 in pattern_states.items():
            for type2, state2 in pattern_states.items():
                if type1 < type2:
                    correlation = np.abs(np.vdot(state1, state2))
                    key = f"{type1}_{type2}"
                    correlations[key] = float(correlation)
                    
        return correlations

    def _generate_pattern_state(self, pattern: Dict) -> np.ndarray:
        """Generate quantum state representation of pattern"""
        state = np.zeros(self.quantum_engine.dimensions, dtype=np.complex128)
        
        if pattern['type'] == 'resonance':
            idx = pattern['dimension']
            phase = pattern['phase']
            state[idx] = pattern['strength'] * np.exp(1j * phase)
        elif pattern['type'] == 'interference':
            pos = int(pattern['resonance_pos'])
            freq = pattern['harmonic_freq']
            for i in range(self.quantum_engine.dimensions):
                state[i] = pattern['strength'] * np.exp(1j * (freq * i + pos))
                
        # Normalize
        state /= np.linalg.norm(state)
        return state

# quantum_interaction_profiler.py

# quantum_interaction_profiler.py

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
import torch
from scipy.spatial import cKDTree
from dataclasses import dataclass
import networkx as nx

@dataclass
class InteractionProfile:
    spatial_patterns: np.ndarray
    quantum_coupling: np.ndarray
    resonance_strength: float
    phase_coherence: float
    entanglement_score: float

class QuantumInteractionProfiler:
    def __init__(self, dimensions: int = 512):
        self.dimensions = dimensions
        self.interaction_fields = np.zeros((dimensions, dimensions, 3), dtype=np.complex128)
        self.coupling_network = nx.Graph()
        self.resonance_cache = {}
        
    def profile_interaction(self,
                          ligand_state: Dict,
                          target_state: Dict,
                          binding_pose: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Generate detailed quantum interaction profile"""
